# Band Arcade: notes for Claude

Mat Graham (middle school band director) owns this project. It's a set of mic-driven practice games for beginning band students, played on school iPads (Safari) and Chromebooks (Chrome) in practice rooms and at home. It's hosted as a static site on GitHub Pages at https://bandarcade.org (the site root; the old `stemband.github.io/band-arcade/` forwards there).

## Hard rules
- Follow docs/STYLE.md for all UI; use the shared components in shared/ui-kit instead of building new versions.
- **No build step, no npm dependencies, no frameworks.** Plain HTML/CSS/JS served as static files. Mat edits and deploys without tooling. The one exception is test tooling: it is allowed ONLY in `tests/` (its own package.json, Playwright as a dev dependency) and in `.github/workflows/`. Nothing in `tests/` is ever loaded by the site (the Pages deploy leaves it out). The one vendored library is three.js **r149** (`shared/vendor/three.min.js` + MIT license), used only by the home page. It's pinned because r149 is the last release with a classic UMD `three.min.js`; later ones are modules-only, which breaks double-click testing.
- **Classic `<script>` tags, not ES modules.** Everything hangs off the `window.Arcade` namespace. Modules break when a page is opened from a local file, and Mat tests by double-clicking.
- **Every address is relative (never a leading `/`, never the domain or `/band-arcade/`)**: the same files must work at the domain root, in a subfolder and from a double-clicked file. Files loaded by shared code find their folder from their own script's address (`document.currentScript.src`: sfx.js, portraits.js, marquees.js, backgrounds.js, avatar-badge.js). The custom domain is set in Settings → Pages (the `CNAME` file is harmless with the Actions deploy). localStorage is per web address: progress moves between addresses only with the Arcade Backup Code.
- **All links end in `index.html`** (e.g. `../index.html`, `ghost-notes/index.html`) so they work both locally and hosted. Build links with `Arcade.link(path)` so `?demo` carries through.
- **Never copy shared logic into a game.** Instruments and transpositions live only in `shared/instruments.js`, pitch detection only in `shared/pitch.js`, and so on. A fix to the engine must reach every game.
- **Always start music and ambience with the music manager: `Arcade.sfx.setMusic(name | [names] | null)` / `setAmbience(...)` (shared/sfx.js). Never start audio directly** (no `new Audio()`, no AudioContext of your own, no play() calls): browsers block audio until the first tap on each page load, and the manager remembers the wished track and starts it as soon as the audio is unlocked AND the file has loaded.
- **Sound while listening mutes the detector.** Every sound goes through `shared/sfx.js` (loaded by EVERY page, after `sounds.js`) and respects the mute and volume settings. A sound played while a game listens to the microphone (`Pitch.listening()`) automatically calls `Pitch.suppress(sound length + 250 ms)`: nothing heard in that window counts as a hit, a wrong note or toward a hold (`onHeld` never fires, `onFrame` gets `null`), and a note still sounding afterwards needs a fresh attack (the `ignoreCurrent` behavior). Every game timer or reaction clock PAUSES during the window (`Pitch.isSuppressed(now)` each frame: Ghost Notes' note clock; Neon Face-Off shows the receiver's note only after `Pitch.suppressedUntil`, stretches the puck so they still get their full window, and freezes the puck + reaction clock during later windows; Vanishing Ink's answer clock (`Echo.create({pauseSuppressed: true})`); its study clock does not pause, since nothing is answered while the ink shows). EXCEPT MUSIC HIGHWAY, whose backing groove plays WHILE listening and never mutes the mic (it plays straight into `Sfx.output()`, never through `Sfx.event`; the drums are unpitched noise + a 70 ms kick, a hit counts only with the right pitch, a snare player's hit on a backing drum hit must beat the learned bleed level; no sound effects during a song), EXCEPT RHYTHM DOJO, whose CLICK (the count-in, and in TAP or headphones mode the whole beat) and the TAP pad's woodblock play straight into `Sfx.output()` through music-highway/backing.js's kit, never through `Sfx.event`, and never mute the mic: claps heard before the rhythm's first note (count-in) never count, and a clap within `bleedMs` of a click (shifted by how late the count-in's clicks were heard: the median of the onsets within `bleedLearnMs` of a click, `clickLag`) must beat `bleedK` × the loudest click-time onset heard during the count-in (both unpitched noise; its "Ready!" `rd-count-in` ends before it listens), and EXCEPT NOTE STORM, which never pauses (see its section): it sets `Sfx.muteMax` (only the first ms of any sound mutes the mic) and `Pitch.softSuppress` (a note started during the window counts as soon as it ends; a note already counted and still held never counts twice). "During play" sounds (`play: true` in sounds.js: note-hit/wrong/missed, life-lost, puck sounds, your-turn…) must be under 0.5 s. Never play a sound per note in the Note Checker (it would deafen the detector mid-scale; only `all-notes-found`). Mic-free games (Note Ninja, Chime Heist, Ancient Ninja Scrolls, Button Masher, Dojo Duel, Keys to the City and Blocktave in touch mode) play freely: no suppression happens when nothing is listening. `mic: false` sounds (the ambience, the floor's) never play while listening.
- **No game plays pitched audio, EXCEPT LOST SIGNAL and MUSIC HIGHWAY's HEADPHONES MODE and PRACTICE MODE** (a guide melody, bass and chords from `music-highway/backing.js`: HEADPHONES quietly, only after the SPEAKER CHECK proved the mic can't hear the speakers; PRACTICE out loud, because the microphone is never requested or used there; its Song Board, which never uses the mic, plays songs with a soft synth). It takes turns: it pauses the mic (`Pitch.pauseListening(true)`) before a pattern plays, listens again only after the last tone has fully faded (tone end + 400 ms) and its "your turn" sound has ended, then `Pitch.ignoreCurrent()`. Its tones come ONLY from `shared/tones.js` (`Arcade.tones`, through sfx.js's output: mute and EFFECTS apply). Every other game still never plays a pitched tone, EXCEPT KEYS TO THE CITY in TOUCH mode (no microphone: every tapped key plays `Sfx.piano(midi)`, which refuses while `Pitch.listening()`; its INSTRUMENT mode plays no pitched sound at all, its effects are unpitched noise), EXCEPT BLOCKTAVE's Conductor's Podium ▶ LISTEN (`Sfx.piano`, which refuses while `Pitch.listening()`), EXCEPT DOJO DUEL, which never uses the microphone, so its own sounds (gong, jingles; all `mic: false`) may be pitched; none may ever play one while listening. EXCEPT TUNE UP's TUNER: HEAR THE NOTE plays ONE 2 s tone through `shared/tones.js`, taking turns like Lost Signal (the mic paused before it, listening again only after it has faded + 400 ms, then `Pitch.ignoreCurrent()`). Tune Up's METRONOME clicks (unpitched noise, backing.js's kit) never play while listening: that tab turns the microphone off. Lost Signal's own sounds are unpitched (noise) and mic: false except two tiny echo clicks; its menu music plays only on its level screens (never during a transmission).
- **THE SITE VERSION (stale-cache fix; `shared/version.js`).** GitHub Pages lets browsers keep files ~10 minutes, so every page's `<head>` starts with `<meta http-equiv="Cache-Control" content="no-cache">` + two lines: `document.write` of `shared/version.js?t=<now>` (fetched fresh every load) and `Arcade.checkVersion('dev')` (an older cached copy of the page reloads once with `?v=<new>`). Copy those three lines into any new page (with the right `../`). Write script/stylesheet tags as plain relative `src`/`href` WITHOUT `?v=`: on every push to main the deploy workflow (`.github/workflows/pages.yml` → `tools/stamp-version.py`, on the deploy's copy, nothing committed) writes the commit's short id into `Arcade.VERSION` and each page's `checkVersion`, and adds `?v=<id>` to every local .js/.css tag. Files loaded by code (pictures, extra scripts) go through `Arcade.v(url)` (portraits.js, marquees.js, arcade.js's three.js, Arcade Quest's art). NEVER edit VERSION by hand: it stays `'dev'` in the repository, which turns all of this off (local files, `python3 -m http.server`). Sounds keep their own `SOUNDS_VERSION`. Needs Settings → Pages → Source: GitHub Actions.
- **THE APP (installable, offline; `manifest.webmanifest`, `sw.js`, `shared/app.js`, `offline.html`, icons in `shared/app/` from `tools/app-icon.html`).** `checkVersion` writes every page's app tags (manifest link, apple-touch-icon, apple-mobile-web-app-* with a black-translucent status bar) and loads `shared/app.js` (`A.ROOT` = the site root from version.js's own address), so a new page needs only the three version lines + its own `theme-color`; `<meta name="arcade-app" content="off">` above them = no app.js (the avatar card, shown inside other sites). `A.v(url)` uses `A.pageVersion` (the page's own checkVersion value), so a kept old page never loads new files. THE SERVICE WORKER (`sw.js`, root, registered by app.js ONLY when VERSION isn't 'dev'; a leftover one is unregistered in dev): one cache per version `band-arcade-<version>`; the deploy (stamp-version.py) writes VERSION + `FILES` (every served file: a content fingerprint + 'p' stored at install: html/js/css/woff2/manifest/icons, or 'u' on first use: sounds, pictures); an install COPIES unchanged files from the older cache, downloads the rest; activate deletes older caches + `clients.claim()`. Pages (navigations) and version.js: network first with `cache: 'no-cache'`, the stored copy only when the network fails, else `offline.html` ("You're offline. Games you've played before still work!"). Everything else: cache first; `?v=<another version>` = network only, never stored (no mixed versions); requests with cache reload/no-cache/no-store or a Range header, other sites (THE LEADERBOARD) and non-GET are never touched. UPDATES (app.js): checked on load, when the page comes back on screen and every 30 min; a waiting worker whose version = this page's is activated silently; an older open page gets the banner `.app-update` "New version ready — tap to update" (hidden `html.in-play`; the tap = skip-waiting + reload); ignored = applied at the next open. INSTALL: `A.App.installButton(el)` (the floor's sound panel) only with `Arcade.TEACHER.SHOW_INSTALL_PROMPT` (teacher-settings.js, false until the final address), `beforeinstallprompt` is always held for it; iPad = the steps with pictures. BRING YOUR PROGRESS: the app's first launch (standalone + nothing in localStorage when app.js runs: `bandarcade.app-welcome` 'ask' → 'done', outside the Backup Code) on the floor, after PRESS START and before pick mode (`A.App.welcome(then)` in arcade.js): paste a Backup Code or Start fresh. `A.App.micHelp()` adds "open Band Arcade in Safari instead" to mic-gate's errors in the app. SAFE AREAS: `.wrap`, `.overlay` and every full-screen layout pad with `env(safe-area-inset-*)`. `?standalone` pretends to be the app (tests); `A.App.state()`. Tests: tests/app.spec.js (stamps two copies with `testv1`/`testv2`).
- Cabinet marquees show only the game's main title, fit as large as possible (titleFit:'max'). No subtitles, taglines or small text on marquees. (shared/marquees.js MARQUEES below: 1 line or 2, measured with the real font, glow and slant.)
- Game MENU BACKGROUNDS show on menu screens only, never during play (shared/backgrounds.js: Mat's picture in shared/backgrounds/ → the coded scene games.js `bg.scene` → the plain page). No flashing: ≤ 3 flashes a second, never full-screen (the storm's lightning is a soft glow behind the clouds).
- Every game opens on a PRESS START title screen (shared/press-start.js, shown by mountTopbar from its games.js entry: every game except tools and `pressStart: false`), because each game is its own page and browsers block sound until a tap there; its tap unlocks + primes the audio and starts the menu music, and never starts the microphone.
- Every game's first screen uses the shared LEVEL SELECT PATTERN (`shared/level-select.js`: SELECT YOUR NOTES, SELECT YOUR LEVEL, then START; `Arcade.LevelSelect.show(...)` each time the level cards are drawn, `LevelSelect.played(i)` in every start function; a setup screen's main button: `LevelSelect.highlight`); new games use it too.
- Games must work on iPad Safari. The mic and AudioContext start only from a tap, via `Arcade.requireMic(fn)`.
- Keep the student-facing copy plain and encouraging. Students are 11–14.
- Band Ninja features are optional. Never add a login or make any game depend on a Band Ninja link, code or PIN.
- **THE LEADERBOARD ENDPOINT IS THE ONLY PLACE THE ARCADE MAY SEND DATA** (`Arcade.LEADERBOARD_URL` in `shared/leaderboard-config.js`, only through `shared/leaderboard.js`), and ONLY these fields: `{pid (random device id), grade, name: [title, adjective, noun] word NUMBERS, game, type ('stars'|'endless'|'play'), value, level}`. Never add names, emails, PINs, typed text or any other personal data, never another endpoint, analytics or third-party script. Nothing is sent in `?demo`, with the student's "Show me on the leaderboard" switch off, without a grade, or when the URL is empty. Every other request the arcade makes is to its own files.

## Page flow
1. `index.html`, the arcade: PRESS START → CHOOSE YOUR INSTRUMENT (pick mode, below) → THE ZONE LOBBY → a ZONE (its cabinet carousel), ALL GAMES or the FULL ARCADE → a game. All one page (arcade.js routes by the address; lobby.js draws the lobby and ALL GAMES), so the audio stays unlocked and the lobby ambience never restarts between views. Every view is a history entry (Back / the iPad back-swipe: game → zone → lobby):
   - LOBBY `index.html` (no hash): (during a SEASONAL EVENT: its banner + decorations first, see SEASONAL EVENTS) one neon sign per zone (games.js `ZONES`: color, name, tagline, small cabinet silhouettes `Arcade.cabSilhouetteSVG`, the zone's stars for the current instrument via `Arcade.gameStars`); 3 columns in landscape, 2 in portrait and on phones (7 zones today + the PRIZE COUNTER's sign last = 8 signs: a lone last sign stands centered, the same size, arcade.css `.zones` 6/4-column grid; a sign's cabinet silhouettes shrink to fit it; the prize sign is `.zsign.zs-prize` with no `data-zone`, see THE PRIZE COUNTER); each sign flickers on once per page load (two gentle dips, staggered, opacity only: measured 0 flashes over the WCAG 0.1 threshold) then hums (a slow glow). The arcade's name (`#arcadeName`, and `#psName` on PRESS START) and the Leaderboard title (the top bar's `#lbBtn` label, the screen's `#lbTitle`) are ALL CAPS by CSS only (`text-transform`, end of arcade.css; the text stays "Band Arcade"); no other text. On top: CONTINUE (the last game opened here: `store.gameData('floor').last`, set by arcade.js when a game opens) and ASSIGNED (`shared/featured.js`, `Arcade.featuredGame()`: `{game, note, until}`, off when null or after `until`). FLAT HTML/CSS ONLY: never three.js here; marquee pictures are `Arcade.Marquee.thumb(g)` still frames (drawn once per page and size from the marquee renderer, redrawn when a font or picture arrives).
   - A ZONE `#zone=<id>[&game=<id>]`: the carousel (◀ ▶, swipe, ←/→, lights, tap a side cabinet) with ONLY that zone's games, no repeats (1 game = no arrows; 2 games = a straight row, not a ring: the second stands right of the first, no wrap-around, the arrow at an end is `disabled`; 3+ = a ring as before), in the zone's `order` (below); the address follows the front cabinet (replaceState). ← LOBBY (`history.back()` when the lobby is under it, else to the lobby), the zone's name in the top bar (`--z` colors). START opens the game (below).
   - ALL GAMES `#all-games`: every floor game once (a game in two zones too), as a card: marquee thumb, name, zone tags, stars, 2P / ASSIGNED badges, and a "No instrument needed" tag (`.ni-tag`) on games.js `noInstrument: true` games. THE FILTER (`#niFilter`, a chip with `aria-pressed` above the cards): "No instrument needed" shows only those games (`#allCount` says how many), remembered for the browser session only (sessionStorage `bandarcade.noinst`). `noInstrument` = playable all the way through with NO LIVE INSTRUMENT (touch, taps, claps, on-screen keys): today Note Ninja, Keys to the City, Chime Heist, Rhythm Dojo, Ancient Ninja Scrolls, Button Masher, Dojo Duel, Blocktave (TOUCH mode) (check a new game's code before setting it: the mic for CLAPS only still counts; needing your instrument's notes anywhere does not). Tests: tests/arcade-exit.spec.js. Scrolls.
   - THE FULL ARCADE `#full-arcade[&game=<id>]` (the top bar's FULL ARCADE button, pink, next to ALL GAMES; hidden in the view itself): the SAME carousel as a zone (arcade.js keeps `current = 'zone'` with `zone = FULL`; `Arcade.Arcade.state().view` = 'full'), with every floor game once (`fullGames()`: zone by zone in `zoneList()` order, each zone's `zoneGames` order, a game in two zones only in its first; games in no zone last; no tools). A zone tag under each cabinet (3D: a small lit plate in front of its base, `opts.tag`/`setRing(…, tag)` in arcade3d.js, layer 1 = no reflection; 2D: `.cab-ztag`), the zone as a tag in the info, and the QUICK JUMP strip (`#jumpStrip`: `Marquee.thumb` buttons grouped by zone, `.jg` with the zone's color line + name; the front game's lit, `aria-current`, scrolled to the middle; a tap = `goTo(i)`, one quick spin; thumbnails filled in a few at a time after the cabinets). The lights are hidden there. Opens on `featuredGame()`, else `Lobby.lastGame()`, else the first game. Back from a game returns to it (sessionStorage `from.view = 'full'`); its Back button goes to where it was opened from.
   - THE TOP BAR (`.fbar`, every view): back, the title, LEADERBOARD (the trophy, when a scoreboard address is set: see THE LEADERBOARD), TUNE UP (`note-checker/index.html`: THE TOOLBOX, see TUNE UP (TOOLBOX) below; games.js `tool: true`, NOT a cabinet; it stays in `A.GAMES` for its Select Player and sounds), ALL GAMES, FULL ARCADE, the AVATAR BADGE (below: EDIT AVATAR / CHANGE INSTRUMENT = pick mode), sound.
   - OPENING A GAME (`openGame`): not suited to the saved instrument (games.js `fit`, `Arcade.gameFit(g, memberId)`) → the `#fitDlg` panel (tag + why + SWITCH INSTRUMENT = pick mode with `forGame`, which dims the unsuited tiles and opens the game after); a two-player game with its own Select Player (`players: 2` without `player`: Neon Face-Off) or no instrument saved → Select Player for that game; else `Sfx.playThenGo('select-<id>', '<id>/index.html')` with the saved instrument. Dimmed games stay visible everywhere with their `fit.tag` (2D `.slot.nofit`, 3D a `fade` factor on the cabinet, cards `.nofit`).
   - BACK FROM A GAME: its "← ARCADE" is `index.html#<game id>`; arcade.js reads sessionStorage `bandarcade.from` (`{view: 'zone'|'all'|'lobby', zone}`, written when the game opened) and shows that zone (or ALL GAMES, or the lobby) with the game in front, putting the lobby underneath in history. The Note Checker's comes back to the lobby.
   PRESS START (`#pressStart`, arcade.js): on the first visit of a browser session (sessionStorage `bandarcade.visit`), a full-screen attract screen (the neon sign, a blinking PRESS START, "TAP ANYWHERE" on touch screens / "PRESS ANY KEY"); it's a real, focused button; any tap/click/key dismisses it with a quick flash, and that same gesture unlocks the audio so the lobby ambience starts at once; then pick mode opens (CONTINUE AS is one tap). Later visits skip both (a device with no instrument gets pick mode once per page load). `?demo&nostart` skips PRESS START for tests.
   PRESS START (`#pressStart`, arcade.js): on the first floor visit of a browser session (sessionStorage `bandarcade.visit`), a full-screen attract screen (the neon sign, a blinking PRESS START, "TAP ANYWHERE" on touch screens / "PRESS ANY KEY"); it's a real, focused button; any tap/click/key dismisses it with a quick flash, and that same gesture unlocks the audio so the lobby ambience starts at once. Later visits skip it (the first tap anywhere still unlocks). `?demo&nostart` skips it for tests.
   A zone's cabinets are drawn in 3D (`arcade3d.js`, three.js, loaded the first time a zone opens) when WebGL works, else in 2D (`shared/cabinets.js`). `?flat` forces 2D; `?keep3d` (testing only) disables the automatic slow-device fallback; `?fps` shows the average frame time. `Arcade.Arcade.state()` = `{view, zone, game, ring, kind}` for tests.
2. SELECT PLAYER = `index.html?game=<id>` (`&players=2` for a two-player game: games.js `players: 2`; `Arcade.playerLink` builds it; `&need=pitched|noplay`), the `#selectView` view of the floor page. PICK MODE = `index.html?pick[=<game id>]` (+ the hash of the view under it) = `SelectView.open(null, {forGame, onDone})`: the instrument for the whole arcade, no game (the arcade's name as its sign, `.sp-view.pick`, select-music, no Player 2); confirming calls `onDone(memberId)` (arcade.js: `history.back()` to the view underneath, then opens `forGame` if it now fits) instead of going to a game. `select-player/player.js` = `Arcade.SelectView.open(game, {players, need})` / `close()` (everything it binds uses one AbortController, so closing removes it all), styles `select-player/style.css` (scoped under `.sp-view`), markup in index.html. arcade.js keeps the page in step with the address (`showView()`): START = `history.pushState` + open; the browser's Back/Forward (popstate) and "← ARCADE" close/open it; links inside the view to another game's Select Player stay on the page. While it's open `body.in-select` hides the floor and `Arcade.floorPaused` stops the 3D drawing. `select-player/index.html?game=<id>` still works: it redirects to `../index.html?game=<id>` with every flag. A fighting-game character select. The game's marquee (small), "CHOOSE YOUR INSTRUMENT" (the heading, `#spTitle`; `text-wrap: balance`, so narrow screens get two even lines; one line in landscape; the page title "Choose Your Instrument · …"; the view is still called Select Player in code and docs; every student-facing name of the screen says Choose Your Instrument). THE ANNOUNCER (player.js `announce`): the `choose-instrument` voice line plays as the view opens, 0.5 s in or when the last effect (the START sound, the coin) has ended (`Sfx.busy()`), whichever is later; only if a tap on this page already started the audio (`Sfx.started`; a page loaded straight onto the view stays quiet, never late); at most once a minute (sessionStorage `bandarcade.choose-at`, set when it plays, so coming back from a game after a minute plays it again); cancelled by a tap on a tile / CONTINUE / SELECT or an arrow/Enter/Space before it starts; held while a panel covers the view ("Create your player?", an UNLOCKED! card, the creator, the LOCKER); the heading swells once with it (`.sp-title.say`, none under reduced motion) and the music dips (`Sfx.duck`). The floor page `Sfx.prefer`s it at load (PRESS START), so it is decoded before the first play, "CONTINUE AS <instrument>" (with its portrait) when one is saved, a big preview + PLAYER CARD (name, key · clef, first five, stars on this device via `store.starsForPlayer`, the horn's "Starting notes: F–C / C–G" toggle = `store.setHornStart`, and a LOCKER button that opens THE LOCKER (see below): player items + this instrument's skins, live preview, locked ones as silhouettes with their requirement), and the 16 instrument tiles (`Arcade.PLAYERS` order; landscape ≥ 900 px: 6 columns × 3 rows with the last 4 centered (a 12-half-column grid, each tile spans 2); portrait and phones: 4 × 4; sizes from the grid + `clamp()`, never fixed px; names wrap, never cut off; in landscape the preview's portrait is sized from the screen height so the card and SELECT fit at 768 px tall; ↑/↓ go to the nearest tile in the row above/below on screen), tinted by family (woodwinds `--pink`, brass `--amber`, percussion `--cyan`). The big preview shows the student's AVATAR (Create Your Player) wearing the highlighted instrument's skins, with the instrument as a badge (`#pvInst`); the card names the player (`#cPlayer`, 1P tag) and has EDIT PLAYER (`#editBtn`: `Arcade.AvatarCreator.open`); CONTINUE AS shows the avatar; the LOCKER previews every item and skin ON the avatar. On a device's first visit Select Player asks "Create your player?" once (`AvatarCreator.offer`, `store.avatarOffered`; skippable: the random avatar stays), before the UNLOCKED! catch-up card (skins + player items). Page overlays go above the view (`body.in-select>.overlay`). Tap highlights (pulsing outline + 1P marker), tap again or SELECT confirms; arrows move, Enter selects. Confirm calls `Arcade.store.setPlayer(memberId)`, flashes "PLAYER 1 READY", then goes to the game. After the members migration it shows `store.pending` (the old group's tiles dashed + "Pick your exact instrument!", or the Colored Tone Bells notice). Two-player: after Player 1 (cyan 1P marker; "PLAYER 1 READY"), "PLAYER 2 — PRESS START": Player 1's tile keeps its 1P marker, Player 2's highlight is magenta with a 2P marker, a CPU tile joins the grid, the preview shows the GUEST avatar (`Avatar.guest()`; the card's buttons become EDIT GUEST + SURPRISE ME), "Same opponent" replaces Continue, and the choice is saved with `store.setOpponent(memberId | 'cpu')` (its own horn setting `opponentHornStart`), never replacing Player 1's instrument. A bad or missing `?game=` goes home.
3. Game pages: `Arcade.requireInstrument(GAME_ID)` returns the saved player's GROUP, or sends students with no saved instrument (member) to Select Player for that game. `Arcade.mountTopbar(inst, extraHTML, GAME_ID)`: "← ARCADE" goes home (turned to this game), the AVATAR BADGE (the avatar, its name, the member's `short` name) whose CHANGE INSTRUMENT opens Select Player for this game (a fixed-instrument game: the floor's pick mode, then the lobby).
- THE SAVED CHOICE IS AN INSTRUMENT MEMBER (`Arcade.store.player`: `'trumpet'`, `'oboe'`, `'horn'`…). Games never ask "Which instrument do you play?": the member IS the answer (`store.memberFor(group)` returns it, `Arcade.currentMember()` gives it). Behind the scenes it maps to its old player GROUP with **`Arcade.groupFor(memberId, {hornStart})`** (instruments.js; `store.instId` uses it), and every game keeps saving progress under the group id, so old stars never move. Always go member → group through that helper. Horn is one member in two groups (`hornF` / `hornC`), picked by `store.hornStart` ('F' default | 'C'). Colored Tone Bells is gone. The one-time `players-v1` migration in storage.js maps old saves (see README "Players and old saves").
- A one-instrument game sets `player: '<group id>'` (and `playerName`) in `games.js` (Chime Heist: `'bells'`); a game with no instrument sets `player: 'all'` (Ancient Ninja Scrolls; progress saved under inst id `'all'`, no label, no modes note). `Arcade.startLink(game, root)` then goes straight to the game (the floor's 2D and 3D START both use it), Select Player redirects there, the saved instrument is never changed, and the floor's hi-score reads that group. Such a game uses `Arcade.getInstrument(id)` instead of `requireInstrument`, and `mountTopbar(inst, extra, GAME_ID, {fixed: 'Bell Kit'})` shows a plain label instead of the instrument link. `noteModes: true` marks a note-reading game (the NOTES × ORDER picker; the floor shows its all-modes total).
- `players: 2` (Neon Face-Off) makes START and the chip open Select Player with `&players=2`.
- ZONES (games.js): `Arcade.ZONES` = the lobby's zones in order `{id (never rename: it's in addresses), name, color (a neon token name), tagline, order?}` (`order`: game ids the zone shows first, the first in front when it opens; Technique Lab starts on Showtime Malfunction, which suits every instrument); each game's `zones: [ids]` (more than one is fine; none = only in ALL GAMES); `auto: '<game flag>'` = the zone holds every floor game with that flag, in games.js order, never listed in `zones` (NO INSTRUMENT NEEDED, `auto: 'noInstrument'`, blue, right after the Band Ninja Dojo: a game flagged later joins by itself and stays in its own zones too; the FULL ARCADE skips auto zones so each cabinet stays once under its own zone; `zonesOf` (ALL GAMES' tags) leaves them out; `A.inZone(g, z)`; nothing in it is dimmed or asked to switch instrument: `gameFit(g, member, zone)`; tests/no-instrument-zone.spec.js); `fit: {only|not: [member ids], tag, why}` = which instruments it SUITS in the lobby (display only: the game's own page still uses `unpitched`/`noPlay`/`player`); `tool: true` = not a cabinet (the Note Checker). Helpers in ui.js: `Arcade.floorGames()` (games minus tools), `zoneById`, `zoneGames(id)`, `zoneList()` (zones with games), `zonesOf(g)`, `gameFit(g, memberId)` → `{ok, tag, why}`, `featuredGame()`, `gameStars(g)` (the current instrument's all-modes stars; a fixed-player game's own). Current fits: Scale Trainer not snare; Keys to the City every instrument (no instrument needed); Music Highway every instrument (the snare plays rhythms); Chime Heist every instrument (no `fit`: it always plays as the Bell Kit, `player: 'bells'`, and never changes the saved instrument, so it is never dimmed and never asks to switch; tests/chime-heist-fit.spec.js); Button Masher + Sustain Speedway not bells/snare ("Winds & brass only"); Ghost Notes, Note Storm, Note Ninja, Neon Face-Off, Lost Signal, Vanishing Ink not snare; the rest every instrument.
- `noPlay: {…, block: true}` (Sustain Speedway: bells + snare can't hold a long tone): `requireInstrument` sends those members to Select Player with `&need=noplay`, which dims their tiles (`.no-play`, via `Arcade.blockedBy(game, memberId)` in ui.js) and shows `noPlay.label` with links to `noPlay.games`; the floor shows the label as a link. Without `block`, `noPlay` only changes the floor's hi-score line.
- `byMember: true` (Button Masher, Neon Face-Off) saves progress under the MEMBER id (`trumpet`, `clarinet`…) of the saved player, and the floor's hi-score reads it. Every hi-score line names the player's member (`short`). `noPlay: {groups, members, label, game}` replaces the hi-score with a link to another game for instruments the game can't use (percussion → Chime Heist).

## Engine API (shared/)
- THE UI KIT (`shared/ui-kit.js` = `Arcade.UI` + `shared/ui-kit.css`; docs/STYLE.md = when to use what; `docs/gallery.html` = every piece and every game's level select / pause / settings / results side by side, pictures from `GALLERY=1` test runs): loaded on EVERY page by shared/version.js (checkVersion writes the two tags, the CSS BEFORE the page's own stylesheets so a game re-themes a piece in its style.css), inert until called. BUTTONS `.btn` + `.btn-primary` (yellow, the ONE main action) / `.btn-secondary` (outlined) / `.btn-danger` (red outline: leaving, starting over) / `.btn-small` (44 px) / `.btn-big` (60 px) (the old btn-gold/btn-ghost names are gone); OPTION TOGGLES `.ui-seg` (buttons with aria-pressed; `--seg-on` recolors); `.ui-switch`; HEADINGS `.game-title` (the kit sets size, the game its font/colors), `.ui-title`, `.ui-section` (THE GOLD SECTION HEADING: ① SELECT YOUR NOTES / ② SELECT YOUR LEVEL / UNLOCKED!), `.ui-kicker`, `.ui-label`, `.ui-howto` (the hub's how-to line); `.ui-tiles`/`.ui-tile` (stat tiles); `.ui-msg` (+ `.err` `.empty` `.loading`). PAUSE `UI.pause.mount({onPause(reason), onResume, onRestart, onLevels, levelsLabel, restartLabel, extras: [{label, onClick, id}], info: () => [[k, v]], note, confirmLeave, leaveText, canPause, pauseOnBlur, place, theme})` → `{setActive(on), pause(), resume(), paused, active, set(o)}`: ONE per page; `setActive(true)` when a level/round/match starts, `false` on every menu; the PAUSE button (`#uiPauseBtn`, ≥ 48 px) sits where "← Arcade" is (measured from `#topbar .brand`, else the top-left corner; `html.ui-in-level` hides that link; `place: el` = inside an element instead: Dojo Duel's center strip), Esc/P, a hidden tab/pagehide (and `pauseOnBlur`) pause; the menu `#uiPause` = RESUME · RESTART · SETTINGS · extras · BACK TO LEVELS (red; `UI.confirm` first when `confirmLeave()`, default true) · BACK TO ARCADE GAMES (`#uiPauseArcade`, `.btn-secondary`, in EVERY game: `arcade: false` hides it, `onArcade()` = the game's own clean-up; the same `confirmLeave` question first, then `Pitch.stop()` (the microphone off for good), `Sfx.hush()` and `playThenGo('ui-back', …)` to the floor's ALL GAMES view turned to this game (`A.homeLink(A.pageGame)` with sessionStorage `bandarcade.from` = `{view: 'all'}`; `A.pageGame` is set by mountTopbar); Dojo Duel's old EXIT is gone: the kit's button replaces it); while paused `Pitch.pauseListening(true)` (the previous state restored on resume, then only a new note counts). RESULTS `UI.results.show({gameId, theme, wide, hero, stars (0–3 | null), kicker, title, msg, tiles, best, newBest, newBestText, extra (html|el), next, retry, levels, more, actsFirst, announce, onShow})` = THE results screen (`#results`, buttons `#resNext` / `#resRetry` / `#resLevels` in that order, LAST, after the game's `extra`; `actsFirst: true` = the buttons row (`.ui-res-acts.ui-res-first`; the overlay gets `.ui-acts-first`) right after the tiles / best line, ABOVE the extra part, and an UNLOCKED! card goes under the buttons (only Sustain Speedway; tests/results-acts-first.spec.js); the first shown of NEXT / TRY AGAIN is yellow and focused; stars pop in one by one (`.ui-stars.go`), the NEW BEST ribbon settles in; it calls `Skins.announce` itself (`announce: false` to skip, or `{members, member}`) and deactivates the pause); `UI.results.hide()`, `.shown`, `render(el, o)` = the same screen inside an element (Dojo Duel's halves). Labels: `Next <noun>`, `Try again` (two-player: `Rematch`), `<Nouns>`. Endless GAME OVER (shared/endless.js) is this screen with `theme: 'ed-over'`, no stars, the Top 5 as its extra. SETTINGS `UI.settings.open({lobby, extras, onClose})` (`#uiSettings`): Sound on/off, Music, Effects (+ the arcade hum on the lobby), Motion, Mic sensitivity (store.sens, `Pitch.setSensitivity`) + the live meter (`Pitch.meterLevel()`, read even while paused; only when a mic is open), then each `UI.settings.register(fn(box), {title, lobby})` group (the game's own options; the lobby's Backup / Install); opened by the top bar's Settings button (`Sfx.mountControls(el, {lobby})`), the pause menu and the lobby; every value is saved on the device and shared by every game. MOTION = `gameData('bg').motion` (the old moving-backgrounds switch): off = still backgrounds and avatars AND `html.no-motion` (ui-kit.css ends every CSS animation/transition at once); scripts check `Arcade.reducedMotion.matches` (shared/version.js: the device's reduce-motion OR the switch; `addEventListener('change')`, `refresh()`), never their own matchMedia. INTRO `UI.intro.show({theme, hero, kicker, title, text, extra, go: {label, onClick}, back: {label, onClick}, onShow})` (`#intro`, `#introGo` = `[data-act=go]`). CONFIRM `UI.confirm({title, text, yes, no, danger, extra, theme, onYes(panel)})` → Promise<bool> (`#uiConfirm`; the safe answer has the focus; Esc = no); `UI.notice({title, text, ok})`; never `alert()`/`confirm()`. TOAST `UI.toast(text, {near, kind, ms})` (always above every panel). PANELS ON TOP (`UI.layer`, ui-kit.js): a panel opened from another panel must never open behind it: `UI.layer.open(el, {min, trap, onEsc, focus})` gives `el` an inline z-index = max(its own, `min`, the highest visible fixed/absolute child of body + 1), remembers the focus, and with `trap` adds the kit's Tab trap + Esc (only the TOP layer reacts) + `body.ui-modal`; `UI.layer.close(el)` clears it and puts the focus back on the opener. Every kit panel (confirm, notice, intro, pause, settings) goes through it, so a confirmation always lands above what asked it; so do Backup / Restore (min 80: above Settings 70 and the leaderboard 70, below confirmations 90 and toasts 95), Create Your Player, the Locker, Sustain Speedway's garage, the lobby's event panel and the app's panels. Never change a panel's look for it; `UI.layer.state()` for tests (tests/backup-layer.spec.js). Every kit panel: role dialog + label, focus in and back out, Tab trapped, Esc, ↑/↓/←/→ between a menu's buttons, keys never reach the game underneath, `body.ui-modal` while one is open (Arcade Quest's keys stop). `UI.state()` for tests.
- `Arcade.INSTRUMENTS`, `Arcade.currentInstrument()`. Each instrument (player group) has `notes` (written), `targetPc` (concert pitch classes), `writtenName(pc)`, `clef`, `t` (transposition), `concertLabel`, `shortName`, and `members`. Never change a group's `id`, first five notes or `t`: saved stars and links depend on them (the flute group's name lost "Colored Tone Bells" when that member was removed).
- UNPITCHED PLAYERS: the Snare Drum is group `snare` / member `snare` (`pitched: false`, no notes, no chromatic range; `inst.pitched` is true for every other group). Only games with `unpitched: true` in games.js take it (Showtime Malfunction, the Note Checker's ARTICULATION mode); `requireInstrument` sends it to Select Player with `&need=pitched` everywhere else ("Snare drummers: try Showtime Malfunction! …", `A.SNARE_MSG`); Select Player dims its tile there (`.no-play`) and refuses it (incl. Face-Off's Player 2 and CONTINUE AS); the floor shows a link to Showtime Malfunction instead of a hi-score. Fixed-player games (Chime Heist, Ancient Ninja Scrolls) are unaffected. Never call scales/sequences for it.
- Members (`inst.members`): the real instruments in a group, `{id, name, short (tile/chip label), family ('woodwind'|'brass'|'percussion'), chromatic: [lowest, highest written], sounds, pitched}`; `Arcade.PLAYERS` = the 16 member ids in Select Player order (woodwinds, then brass, bells, snare), `Arcade.memberById(id)`, `Arcade.groupsOf(id)`. `sounds` = exact semitones the instrument sounds BELOW written pitch (negative = higher; bells −24, tenor sax 14), octave included, always ≡ `t` mod 12. Ranges come from the **GMEA All-State Middle School Chromatic Scale sheets**; if GMEA changes a sheet, update `MEMBERS` in `instruments.js` (not a game). Helpers: `Arcade.getMember(inst, id)`, `Arcade.chromaticScale(member, {down})` (written notes with `midi` and `sounding`; sharps going up like the GMEA sheets, flats going down, end notes keep their given spelling), `Arcade.music.spell(midi, flats)`.
- `Arcade.Pitch.setInstrument(inst)`, `onFrame((reading, level, now) => …)` (~25×/s; `reading` is `null` or `{freq, midi, note, pc, cents}` with a concert `pc` and `note` = rounded SOUNDING midi), `onHeld((pc, now, note) => …)` (once per held note, `holdMs` = 280), `ignoreCurrent()`, `heldPc()`, `setSensitivity(0–100)`, `bars(level)`, `levelPct(level)`.
- ATTACKS (opt-in; only pages that subscribe run it): `Arcade.Pitch.onAttack(a => …)`, `a = {time, pc|null, midi|null, level, rise}` (`rise` = the envelope over the quietest point of the last `dipMs`, null for a jump or a ?demo key: information only) once per new articulation (tongued note, mallet strike, drum hit). A separate small analyser is read every 5–8 ms; its window follows the instrument (`ENV.periods` cycles of the lowest note, 256–2048 samples, resized by setInstrument/setRange). An attack = a rise of the loudness envelope after a dip (`ENV.rise` × the quietest point of the last `dipMs`), or a quick jump (`ENV.jump` × the level `jumpMs` ago) that also climbs `ENV.above` × the loudest point 40–120 ms ago (a bell restruck while ringing); gated by `gateK` × the sensitivity gate; `refractoryMs` 90; never during `suppress`. Its pitch = two agreeing detector readings 50–170 ms after (else the most common, else null). Slurs and vibrato must never fire: tune `Arcade.Pitch.ENV` only against the Note Checker's ARTICULATION test (and ask Mat). `?demo` when a page sets `Pitch.demoAttacks = true`: tap Space = an attack on `Pitch.demoTarget()` ({pc, midi} or null), tap W = a wrong one, hold S = a steady note with no attacks (`Pitch.demoHeld()`).
- `Arcade.Pitch.stop()`: the microphone off for good (tracks stopped, its AudioContext closed, `active` false): the pause menu's BACK TO ARCADE GAMES calls it before the page changes; `start()` again needs a tap.
- `Arcade.Pitch.pauseListening(on)`: a page that listens only at certain moments (Arcade Quest) pauses analysis; while paused `listening()` is false, no readings/holds/attacks, and resuming needs a fresh attack. STALE SOUND (pitch.js `H.stale`): a sound already ringing when the page resets the detector never counts as a new note: after `setRange(...)`, or `pauseListening(false)` with a sound really in the mic at that moment (one analysis on the spot), ANY sound continuing without a gap (110 ms of nothing heard) is ignored (a new range can misread the old sound a half step off), after `ignoreCurrent()` that same pitch (a slur to another pitch still counts). Before this, a reset forgot the ringing note and the next reading fired `onHeld` as brand new: Neon Face-Off showed "That's <the other player's note>" (spelled for the wrong player) whenever no hit sound muted the mic (tests/faceoff-notes.spec.js).
- `Arcade.Pitch.suppress(ms)`: hear nothing new for ms (sfx.js calls it for every sound while listening); what is still sounding afterwards stays counted, so only a new attack fires. `Pitch.softSuppress = true` (a page option, Note Storm only): nothing counts during the window, but a note started inside it counts right after it. `Sfx.muteMax` (ms, a page option, default null) caps how much of a sound mutes the mic (+ the 250 ms echo). `Pitch.suppressedUntil`, `Pitch.isSuppressed(t)` (games pause their clocks while true), `Pitch.listening()` (mic running, or `?demo` standing in). `Pitch.instrument()` / `Pitch.range()` report what the detector is listening for (tests).
- `Arcade.Pitch.a4` (default 440): the reference A every reading is measured against. Only Tune Up's Tuner changes it (Settings → Tuner → Reference A, 438–445, `gameData('tuneup').a4`) while its tab is open, and puts 440 back when it closes; no game ever sets it.
- `Arcade.Pitch.setRange(lowMidi, highMidi)` (Note Checker scale/chromatic modes, and games with a scale or Chromatic NOTES pool via `ModePicker.useRange`; First 5 never calls it): search a member's SOUNDING range, octave-exact holds (D4 then D5 fires twice), and a reading outside the range whose octave above/below is inside is reported in that octave (`reading.folded`), because built-in mics often hear low brass an octave off. For low ranges it averages samples first (÷2–4) so tuba E1 (41 Hz) costs less than a game's tuba range. `setRange(null)` restores the default. `?demo`: `Pitch.demoNote = <sounding midi>` "plays" a note (at loudness `Pitch.demoLevel`, default 0.1), wobbling by `Pitch.demoJitter` semitones (default 0.2 = ±10 cents; Sustain Speedway sets 0.01 for "perfectly in tune").
- Scales (`shared/scales.js`, every game + the Note Checker + the home page; + THE AUDITION table for Scale Trainer, see its section): `Arcade.Scales.LIST` (`Bb`, `Eb`, `F`, `Ab`, `chrom`), `Scales.build(member, id)` → `{name, label ('Concert E♭ (your F Major)'), key, sig {type, count}, up, notes (up then down)}`, each note `{letter, acc, oct, midi (written), sounding, pc, show}` where `show` is how to draw it under the key signature. Written scales are transposed with the member's `sounds` and spelled in the written key (never mixed sharps/flats). The **`START` table** at the top of scales.js holds the written starting note for every member × scale; it should match the GMEA scale sheets, and a wrong-key entry falls back to the rule (B♭ = the first-five tonic; others = the tonic nearest that whose octave fits the GMEA range). `Scales.sequence(scale, count)` repeats the scale to fill a level; `Scales.progressKey(gameId, id)` = `'<gameId>:scale-<id>'`.
- Portraits (`shared/portraits.js`, loaded by every page with a top bar and by Select Player): the drawn fallback is `Arcade.portraitSVG(memberId, {size: 'big'|'tile'|'chip', color, glow, style: 'neon'|'line', cls, label})`, neon line art on a 100 × 100 grid built from parts (`tube` centerlines drawn as two glowing edges, `shape` outlines, `line`s, `ring`s); each instrument's signature color is a `--pt-<id>` token in theme.css. Color and style are options so a skin can restyle them; never hard-code a color in a part. The same drawing is used big, on tiles and in the chip.
- AVATARS (CREATE YOUR PLAYER): the player is shown as the student's own AVATAR everywhere (Select Player's preview + card + CONTINUE AS, every top-bar chip, every results screen, Button Masher's fighter, Neon Face-Off's players, Sustain Speedway's HUD, Arcade Quest's hero); INSTRUMENTS are still shown with `portraitHTML` (the tiles, the preview's badge, Chime Heist's chip, CPU rivals). One avatar per device, saved in `Arcade.store.avatar` (`setAvatar`; `guestAvatar`/`setGuestAvatar` = Neon Face-Off's Player 2; `avatarOffered`), versioned (`v`), part of the Arcade Backup Code (it's in `exportAll`). A random one is made on the first visit (`Avatar.get()`). Files (load after skins.js, on every page that shows a player): `shared/avatar-parts.js` (`window.AVATAR_PARTS`: every part as pixel maps + the color lists; HOW TO ADD A PART is at its top), `shared/avatar-names.js` (`window.AVATAR_NAMES`: the name builder's word lists, Mat edits them: APPEND-ONLY, every word's place is its permanent number (the leaderboard and the share code use it), retire a word as `'#Word'`; the safety rules for adding words are at its top, plus the NEVER USE list `never` (Zesty, Pickle, Spicy, Wobbly, Director, Nova: student slang/insults, never add them)), `shared/avatar.js` (`Arcade.Avatar`: `get/set/guest/setGuest/random({keep, only})/randomName/normalize/nameOf/bustURL/sprites(member, {avatar, eq})/redrawAll/stampResults/words(k)/nameNote`, `Arcade.avatarHTML({size, member, avatar, guest, skin, label, cls, live, bg})`), `shared/avatar-bg.js` (right after avatar.js, with `bg-scenes.js` on the page: AVATAR BACKGROUNDS, below), and `shared/avatar-creator.js` (loaded by the floor page, Dojo Duel and Arcade Quest; any other page gets it on demand through `Arcade.AvatarBadge.load()`) (`Arcade.AvatarCreator.open({guest, member, onClose})`, `offer({onDone})`) + `shared/avatar.css` (the floor page also loads `shared/instrument-sprites.js` for the full-body preview). Fields: `skin` (12 tones), `face`, `eyes`, `eyeColor`, `brows`, `mouth`, `freckles` (P.FRECKLE_STYLES: false/true/cheeks/nose/dusting; old true/false saves still work), `paint` (face paint: none/stripes/bolt/star, drawn on the cheeks) + `paintColor`, `hair` (26 styles: P.HAIRS, several drawn with the SHAPE KIT near the top of avatar-parts.js: `kit(w)` spans/disc/px/sym, textures coil/twist/rows/wave, `retex`, `shape()`, `CAP`) + `hairColor` (natural + fun + bright), `head` (none/hijab/headwrap/turban/patka/kufi/tichel/durag/beanie/cap/shako/headphones + unlockables) + `headColor`, `top` + `topColor`, `bottom` + `bottomColor`, `shoes` + `shoeColor`, `glasses` + color, `aids` (hearing aids, by the student's own ears) + color, `chair` (wheelchair) + color, `hand` (P.HANDS: a HELD item in the bust's right hand + the sprite's; `bust(api, f)` draws it), `effect` (P.EFFECTS) + `effectColor` (the aura's), `plate` (P.PLATES: the NAME PLATE under the name on results and in the Endless Top 5, `.av-plate-*` in theme.css, `Avatar.plateAttr`), `belt` (OFFICIAL BAND NINJA GEAR: a belt code only, see THE BAND NINJA CONNECTION), `bg` (the background, below), `name` {title, adj, noun}. Every field's ids are also in `shared/avatar-code.js` TABLE (append a new one there). NO free text anywhere: names are TITLE + ADJECTIVE + NOUN (no initial any more). The builder's words = `Avatar.words('title'|'adj'|'noun')`: A–Z, no repeats, NEVER-USE words filtered out even if one is added to a list. NAME MIGRATION (avatar.js `cleanName`/`migrateName`): a saved name with a NEVER-USE word or an old initial is fixed when it's read (the word → a random one from the same list, the initial dropped) and saved; the device's own avatar then gets ONE note, `gameData('avatar').nameNote` 'new' → 'shown': the first avatar badge on the next page shows "Your name got an upgrade! Tap your name to change it." (tap = the creator on the NAME tab; the creator shows it too if it opens first). LONG NAMES (the longest is "Grandmaster Thundering Metronome"): every place a name shows wraps it into balanced lines between words (`text-wrap: balance`, `overflow-wrap: break-word`), never an ellipsis or a cut; Dojo Duel's narrow scoreboards (≤ 600 px) leave it to each half's header. No boy/girl choice. Head coverings hide hair (`hides: 'all'` = hijab/turban; `'top'` = hair only below `clip`). TWO RENDERINGS from the same parts, cached (redrawn only when the avatar changes), crisp pixels: the PORTRAIT BUST (36 × 36, in a `.pt-box.av-box`, so every COLOR skin effect works on it: rim glow, aura, backdrop, sparkle… the animatronic skins recolor its eyes) and the FULL-BODY SPRITE (32 × 32, Arcade Quest and the FIGHT POSES below; layers back to front: legs or wheelchair + seated legs → hair/hood/cape behind → back arm → instrument 'back' parts → body/head → instrument → hands → front arm → 'top' parts; arms from `QUEST_ART.SHOULDERS` to each POSE's hand points; sleeves by top: short 0.35, long 1; the skin's color is its outline; a wheelchair ROLLS (the spokes turn) where others walk). ACCESSORY skins (Headband, Shades, Visor, Crown, Cape, Helmet, Ninja Mask, Baton) are drawn ON the avatar as pixels (`AVATAR_PARTS.ACCESSORIES`); the color/accessory worn is still per instrument (`Skins.equipped(member)`), and the creator's EXTRAS tab lets students wear the ones they've unlocked (saved with SAVE). The creator (`open({guest, member, onClose, tab})`): preview (bust + sprite holding the instrument; tap to turn; the bust's background MOVES; tapping the name opens NAME), tabs FACE · HAIR · HEAD · CLOTHES · EXTRAS · HELD ITEM · PETS · EFFECTS · BACKGROUND · NAME (+ plate) (moving items get the wave icon `ANIM_ICON`; NEW! dots on tabs and items with an unlocked item not yet viewed: `gameData('avatar').viewed`, marked when the tab opens), labeled option buttons (thumbnails / swatches), SURPRISE ME (all; keeps the wheelchair choice), SHUFFLE THIS TAB, UNDO, CANCEL, DONE (saves; fires window `arcade:avatar`, which the badges, Dojo Duel's and Neon Face-Off's setups and Arcade Quest's title listen to); `avatar-open` as it opens (+ `Sfx.duckHold`: the music dips while it's open), `avatar-save` on DONE, `ui-back` on CANCEL; ←/→ tabs, arrows move in a grid, Enter picks, Esc closes (asks if unsaved); z-index 80; `body.avc-open` (Arcade Quest's keys stop). Every results screen gets the avatar + name (`Skins.announce` calls `Avatar.stampResults`; a panel that already shows the avatar gets the name only). THE INSTRUMENT SPRITES (`shared/instrument-sprites.js`, moved out of arcade-quest/sprites.js: `QUEST_ART.SHOULDERS`, `INSTRUMENT_PALETTE`, `SHAPES`, `POSES`; arcade-quest/sprites.js keeps only Arcade Quest's own pictures and adds them to the same `window.QUEST_ART`): load it on any page that draws a full-body avatar (the floor, Dojo Duel, Arcade Quest, Button Masher; avatar-badge.js loads it for the creator). THE FIGHT POSES (any game; Button Masher's fighter): `Avatar.fightSprites(member, {avatar, eq})` → `{w, h (FIGHT_W × FIGHT_H = 44 × 37, the figure at 6, 5), poses: {idle, strike, hit, dizzy, ko, victory, bow}: {frames, fps}, pet, chair}`, built from the same layers as the full-body sprite with the LOWER part (legs, or the wheelchair + seated legs) and the UPPER part (everything else, moving together so nothing floats) shifted by whole pixels, facing right: IDLE carry pose + wide stance + bob (2 fps); STRIKE the playing pose, lunging (bells/snare: mallets/sticks up, then down; 4 fps); HIT pushed back + a small steady impact burst; DIZZY swaying + three stars circling the head; KO a cartoon sit-down on the floor, the instrument set down behind, stars (a WHEELCHAIR user stays in the chair: slumped + stars); VICTORY a fanfare (playing, jumping or, seated, bouncing in the chair, two little notes in the family color rising); BOW front view, a slow bow. A wheelchair user is seated in every pose. Held items aren't drawn on full-body sprites (the hands hold the instrument), as in Arcade Quest. Cached per avatar + instrument (100–160 ms once at 4× CPU throttle). `shared/avatar-fight.js` (`.avf-*` in theme.css; after avatar-fx.js): `Arcade.AvatarFight.mount(el, {member, avatar, pet, effect})` → `{set(pose), pose, react('cheer'|'worry'), mouth(), destroy()}` = ONE live fighter (each pose's own fps, frames copied onto one small canvas; nothing while hidden; reduced motion = the first frame only), the avatar's EFFECT as still pictures in a square around the figure, the PET alone in the player's corner (hops on `cheer`, shivers on `worry`), no avatar background; `stillHTML(member, pose)` = a small still copy (`.av-box`, so `stampResults` adds only the name); `blastHTML(member)` = the neon SOUND-WAVE blast (three arcs, woodwinds pink, brass amber, percussion cyan). Every pose's per-pixel flash rate is ≤ 2 a second. Colors: `--av-*` tokens in theme.css. Fields `pet` (P.PETS: floats beside the bust and the sprite on its own layer at `P.PET_AT`, with its own `pal`, bobbing) and `back` (P.BACKS: `behind` maps, over the body in the back view).
- AVATAR BACKGROUNDS (the `bg` field; `P.BGS` at the end of avatar-parts.js; drawn by `shared/avatar-bg.js`, `.av-bg` in theme.css): a layer at the bottom of every avatar `.pt-box` (under skin backdrops). 14 free ones (none, 5 solids, 3 fades, patterns stripes/dots/staff/checker/stars) and 23 unlockable ANIMATED SCENES = the menu backgrounds' own art from bg-scenes.js (`scene`), drawn brighter (`lift` 1–2: the frame drawn on itself with 'lighter'): 14 per game (clear its final level; Music Highway: a star on its last song; Neon Face-Off / Dojo Duel `{game, wins: 5}`; Arcade Quest `ep1`), 5 star milestones (50/100/150/250/400 device-wide ★), 4 Token Booth ones (bubbles 150, fireflies 200, lavalamp 250, confetti 300; in `QUEST_V2.cosmetics`). `Arcade.AvatarBg.html(id, size, {live})`: chip (the top-bar badge) = just `main`, the one color; tile/big = a cached still frame (`stillURL(id, px)`, JPEG data URL); `live: true` (the creator's preview, Choose Your Instrument's preview, the Locker's, `stampResults`) adds a canvas. ONE background moves at a time: the LARGEST visible live one (covered ones don't count: `elementFromPoint`), ≤ 30 fps, ≤ 320 px, nothing while hidden; still frames with reduced motion, the MOVING BACKGROUNDS switch off (`gameData('bg').motion`), or a slow device (average draw > 14 ms over 90 frames, for the rest of that page). New scenes (city, synthwave, galaxy, records, bubbles, lavalamp, confetti, fireflies) follow bg-scenes.js's rules, so a menu can use them too; test a new one with the flash check (≤ 10 % of the box crossing 0.1 relative luminance in a frame) and a 4× CPU throttle. `AvatarBg.stats()` for tests. Dojo Duel's victory and Neon Face-Off's two-player results show the UNLOCKED! card (`Skins.catchUp`) when a player item is new (their wins unlock backgrounds).
- AVATAR ANIMATION (animated items + EFFECTS; `shared/avatar-fx.js`, loaded right after avatar-bg.js on every avatar page): an item is animated by `anim.pal` (4-frame color cycles), `anim.maps` (4 maps per view) or its `bust(api, f)` (hands), a pet by `frames` + `seq` (the rules are in avatar-parts.js's UNLOCKABLE ITEMS comment). `Avatar.bustFrames(av, eq)` = 4 cached bust canvases (1 when nothing moves), `isAnimated(av, eq)`; sprites get 4 frames too (never with reduced motion). `avatarHTML` marks a box that can move (`data-live`, `LIVE` registry, `liveInfo`) with `.av-live` + `.av-fx` (back/front) canvases over still images (`.av-fx-still`, `AvatarFx.stillURLs`). THE SAME LOOP as the backgrounds (avatar-bg.js) animates ONE box: the largest visible, uncovered `data-live` box (body frames at 4 fps, the effect up to 30 fps) and stops for reduced motion, MOVING BACKGROUNDS off, a slow device or a hidden tab; every other copy is still. Effects (`AvatarFx.FX`: notes, sparkles, orbit, aura (+ the avatar's rim, pre-drawn per frame and color), snow, bubbles, sparks, confetti (bursts once inside a results panel `.av-res`)) never draw over the FACE rect (x 8.5–27.5, y 9.5–25.5 of the 36 grid) and never over the name. PHOTOSENSITIVITY: no item or effect may flash (≤ 3 a second; fades, never blinks); check a new one with a per-pixel flash count and a 6× CPU throttle in the creator with a background + effect + pet + animated item on. `AvatarFx.state(box)` and `AvatarBg.stats().fx` for tests.
- AVATAR ITEM UNLOCKS: a part with `unlock` in avatar-parts.js (the UNLOCKABLE ITEMS section near its end: the rules and HOW TO ADD AN ITEM) is locked until earned; no `unlock` = free. Rules: `{stars: n}` = DEVICE-WIDE stars (`store.allStars('*')`: every progress key, every instrument, every game) at 10/25/50/100/150/200/250/300/400/500/600/750/1000; `{game, level, stars, text}` (+ `suffix` / `exact`: only keys ending in it / only the plain game key) / `{game, achievement, text}` / `{game, badge, text}` / `{game, wins, text}` (matches won on this device: `Skins.winsOn(game)`, skins.js `WINS`: Dojo Duel = the dojo record, Neon Face-Off = h2h + `cpuWins`, old wins count) / `{game, badges: n}` (that many scroll badges) / `{game, perfect: n}` (3 ★ on levels 1..n, any key) / `{game, endless: n}` (an Endless run reached n notes: `Skins.endlessBest(game)` = `store.endlessRuns` + `gameData('endless-best')`, which Endless records outside `?demo`) = `Skins.ruleMet` (saved progress, any instrument); `{tool: 'tuner-hold' | 'ladder', n, text}` = TUNE UP practice (skins.js `toolCount`, gameData('tuneup'): the most HOLD IT rings filled on one day / Tempo Ladders of 8+ steps climbed to the goal; the Golden Tuning Fork (hand `tuningfork`, n 5) and the Ladder Climber name plate (plate `ladder`, n 3); never sold, no stars); `{bandninja: belt}` = that belt's code entered (OFFICIAL BAND NINJA GEAR); `{bandninja: 'all'}` = ALL 10 belt codes entered (`BandNinja.hasAll()`; progress "n of 10 belt codes so far"): the LEGENDARY Grandmaster's Aura (effect `grandmaster`, `official` + `legendary: true`: a slow belt-colored rainbow glow + a crown of 10 tiny diamonds orbiting above the head, avatar-fx.js `FX.grandmaster`; never sold; a LEGENDARY UNLOCKED! card `.sk-legendary`, a gold/diamond frame + LEGENDARY tag on its Locker (`.sk-legend`) and creator (`.avc-legend`) tiles; only `legendary` items get these); `{shop: price}` (50–500) = bought for Arcade Tokens at THE PRIZE COUNTER or Arcade Quest's Token Booth (one shared wallet: shared/tokens.js; `store.ownItem('<field>:<id>')`, owned forever, `store.ownedItems`; an owned key also opens an earned item, which is how save codes carry them); `{shop, booth: 'quest'}` = THE MANOR COLLECTION, sold only at Arcade Quest's booth (set by avatar-parts.js's `MANOR_COLLECTION` list, see THE PRIZE COUNTER). IDENTITY ITEMS ARE NEVER LOCKED, whatever a rule says (avatar.js `IDENTITY`): head none/hijab/headwrap/turban/patka/kufi/tichel/durag, hearing aids, the wheelchair, glasses (+ their colors). `Arcade.Avatar`: `isUnlocked(field, id)`, `requirement(field, id)` ("Earn 150 ★", "250 tokens at the Token Booth", or the rule's `text`), `progress`, `items()` (every lockable item `{key, field, id, name, unlock, shop}`), `freshItems()` / `markSeen(list)` (`store.itemsSeen`/`markItemsSeen`; never bought ones), `effective(av)` (a locked choice falls back to a free one: `get()`/`guest()` return it), `random()` picks only unlocked items. Checked from saved progress (retroactive): `Skins.announce` (every results screen) and `Skins.catchUp` (Select Player) put items on the same UNLOCKED! card (`{item}` entries, **Wear it** sets the avatar field) and play `item-unlocked`. The creator shows locked options as dark silhouettes + a lock + the requirement (`.avc-locked`, not selectable: it says what it takes; a background shows its own dimmed picture, `.avc-bglock`, + progress for star and win goals). New {shop} items also go at the END of `QUEST_V5.cosmetics` in backup.js (QUEST_V1–V4 are frozen). TUMBLERS (hand items, avatar-parts.js TUMBLERS section: a generic insulated cup with a lid, straw and a handle facing out; no brand or brand shape): Neon Pink / Arctic Blue / Lime Tumbler {shop: 150}, Sunset Ombré Tumbler {stars: 250}, Galaxy Tumbler {shop: 400, slow sparkle}, Diamond Tumbler (official, the Diamond belt code; on the BAND NINJA tab's IN YOUR HAND group). EVERY GAME has at least one item (a final level, all 3 ★, an Endless goal, wins, a badge or an achievement). `?demo&unlockall`: everything open (never saved, never announced).
- SEASONAL EVENTS (`shared/seasons.js`, loaded after skins.js on every page with skins; `season-lobby.js` on the floor; styles `.ev-*` in arcade.css): EVENT ITEMS ARE EARNED, SEASONAL SHOP ITEMS ARE BOUGHT: NEVER MIX THEM (an event's gift and ladder items, `unlock: {event}`, are never sold at either counter; the seasonal shop's own items, `unlock: {shop, season}`, are never a gift or a ladder step: see THE PRIZE COUNTER). Limited-time windows when special avatar items can be EARNED; earned items are owned forever (`store.ownItem`, like the Token Booth's) and wearable all year; each event returns every year. THE CALENDAR = `Arcade.SEASONS` at the top of seasons.js (Mat edits it): `{id (never rename), name, emoji, start, end ('MM-DD' every year, inclusive, device's local date, may cross New Year; 'YYYY-MM-DD' = a one-time event such as the commented Concert Week example, which wins over a yearly one on the same days), colors [main, accent] (tokens), deco, jingle, gift: '<field>:<id>', ladder: [{do: 'stars'|'levels'|'games'|'endless'|'days', n, item}] (3–5 steps)}`. Events: Spooky Season 10-01→11-01, Winter Fest 12-01→01-07, Friendship Week 02-07→02-14, Music In Our Schools Month 03-01→03-31, Spring Bloom 04-01→04-30, Summer Send-Off 05-01→05-22. THE ITEMS are parts in avatar-parts.js's SEASONAL ITEMS section with `unlock: {event: '<id>'}` (avatar.js: owned = unlocked; `requirement` = "Spooky Season: Play 3 different games" during it, "Returns next Spooky Season!" otherwise; never sold at the Token Booth; identity items are never event items) and in avatar-code.js TABLE. PROGRESS = THE DAILY ACTIVITY LOG in storage.js (`store.activity` `{'YYYY-MM-DD': {s stars, c levels cleared, g {game: 1}, e best Endless score, p plays}}`, 400 days, in the Arcade Backup Code): `setLevel(game, inst, lvl, p, run)` (every game passes `run` = THIS play's stars: a level with ≥ 1 is cleared), `addEndless` (score), `noteActivity({game, play})` (LevelSelect.played, Dojo Duel / Neon Face-Off match start, an Arcade Quest battle). An event's progress = the log's days inside its dates, so only what happens during it counts and every year starts fresh. `Seasons.check()` earns finished steps (the running event + one that ended ≤ 14 days ago); skins.js calls it before every UNLOCKED! card (results screens, Select Player), so the card celebrates it (its line: "🎃 Spooky Season: yours forever"). The FREE GIFT is claimed in the lobby's EVENT PANEL (`gameData('seasons').claimed[occurrence key]`). THE LOBBY (season-lobby.js): the banner ("🎃 SPOOKY SEASON: 6 days left!", FREE GIFT! / "2 of 5"; a `countdown: true` event: "🎻 Concert in 5 days!" … "Concert tonight!", `Seasons.leftText`) above the lobby cards opens the EVENT PANEL (gift + CLAIM, the ladder with progress bars, every item previewed on the student's avatar, the SEASONAL LOOK switch); the event's jingle (sounds.js `event-<id>-jingle`, built-in fallback) plays on the banner tap and CLAIM only; a touch on each sign (`.lobby[data-deco]`, the event's `deco`) shows with the look. TESTING: `?season=<id>` previews an event today (banner, panel, look, items; progress = today's activity; claims/earned items/seen cards in sessionStorage only: nothing saved); `?demo&today=YYYY-MM-DD` pretends that date (saved normally); `?demo&unlockall` opens every item. `Arcade.Seasons` (`active/steps/progress/claim/check/requirement/owned/occurrences/look/backdrop/lookOn/setLookOn/leftText`) and `Arcade.SeasonLobby.state()` for tests.
- SEASONAL LOOKS (`season-look.js`, the floor page only; styles "SEASONAL LOOKS" at the end of arcade.css; a few `--sl-*` tokens in theme.css): while a season runs, the arcade's MENUS get a full themed backdrop: the lobby, a zone, ALL GAMES, the FULL ARCADE (2D and 3D), Choose Your Instrument and PRESS START; NEVER a game page. WHICH look = `Seasons.look(date)`: the running EVENT's `look` (every event has one: spooky, winter, friendship, miosm, spring, summer; a concert: concert), else a BACKGROUND-ONLY SEASON's (`Arcade.SEASON_BACKDROPS` in seasons.js, Mat edits it: `{id, name, look, dates: [['MM-DD', 'MM-DD'], …]}`: Back to School 08-01→09-30 (school), Fall Harvest 11-02→11-30 (harvest), Frost 01-08→02-06 + 02-15→02-28 (frost)); an event always wins. CONCERT SEASON = the commented template at the end of `Arcade.SEASONS` (one-time 'YYYY-MM-DD' dates, `end` = the concert day, `look: 'concert'`, `countdown: true`, challenges without items). THE SWITCH "Seasonal look: On / Off" (`Seasons.lookOn()`/`setLookOn(on)`, `gameData('seasons').look`, ON by default, fires window `arcade:seasonlook`) is in the shared Settings panel (ui-kit.js, on any page with seasons.js) and the event panel; it REPLACED the DECORATIONS switch: an old `deco: false` reads as Off, and setting it deletes `deco`. Off = the normal arcade everywhere (the banner, panel and items still work). `?season=<id>` previews an event, a background-only season or a look by name (`?season=concert`), nothing saved, even with the switch off. THE DRAWING: one `.slook` (`sl-sky` gradients, `sl-p` props = small inline SVGs at the edges, `sl-fx` a few drifting bits, `sl-dim` darkening the middle so every text keeps its contrast) behind the floor (before `.room`; `html[data-slook]` makes the body transparent and drops the room's haze; the 3D canvas is see-through) and a copy inside `#pressStart` and `#selectView`; strips along the top (string lights, bunting) are fixed-shape pieces in a row, never stretched. 3D: `SeasonLook.palette()` → the haze and side lights' tokens, `Floor3D.look()` applies it (again on a change). MOTION: transforms/opacity only, slow (≥ 4 s cycles; lights breathe a strand at a time, never blink); still with reduced motion or MOTION off (`html.slook-still`). No `filter`, no pictures, no SVG ids (each scene is drawn three times). A new look = a `LOOKS` entry in season-look.js + its colors in arcade.css + its id in `Seasons.LOOK_IDS`. `Arcade.SeasonLook.state()` for tests: tests/seasonal-looks.spec.js.
- THE LEADERBOARD (`shared/leaderboard-config.js` + `shared/leaderboard.js`, loaded after seasons.js on every page that saves progress; `leaderboard-screen.js` on the floor, the top bar's trophy `#lbBtn`; `.lb-*` in arcade.css; README "Leaderboard & privacy"): Mat's Apps Script scoreboard. API: GET `?action=status` → `{ok, enabled}`; GET `?action=board&grade=6|7|8` → `{ok, enabled, grade, week, updated, boards: {stars, improved, streak, endless: {<gameId>: [...]}}}`, entries `{id (6 chars), name: [t, a, n], value}`; POST (Content-Type text/plain: no CORS preflight) `{pid, grade, name, game, type, value, level}`. fetch with `credentials: 'omit'`, redirects followed (Apps Script → googleusercontent), SIMPLE CORS REQUESTS ONLY (no preflight: Apps Script can't answer one): GET with no headers, POST with only `Content-Type: text/plain`, and NO `cache` option (WebKit has turned a non-default cache mode into Cache-Control/Pragma request headers); never blocks a game. A COLD SCRIPT can take 10–20 s: `Leaderboard.TIMEOUTS` = {read: 25000, send: 20000} ms, a board read that times out is tried ONCE more; WARM-UP `Leaderboard.warm()` = one quiet `?action=status` (result ignored, never in ?demo, at most once in 10 minutes: sessionStorage `bandarcade.lb-warm`), called by arcade.js when the floor opens, when the lobby shows and when the page comes back on screen on the lobby. THE LAST GOOD BOARD of each grade is kept in localStorage `bandarcade.lb-last` (not progress, not in the Backup Code): a failed read returns it `{ok, data, stale: true, at, why}`. `Leaderboard.lastRequest()` = `{ok, why ('timeout after 25 s' | 'offline' | 'HTTP 500' | 'not JSON' | 'network error (CORS or blocked)'), ms, tries, at, action}` (warm-ups don't count). EVENTS (storage.js calls them): `stars` = a level's best stars INCREASE (setLevel), `endless` = the score at game over when it beats this device's best this week for that game (addEndless), `play` = once a day (noteActivity with `play`). A QUEUE on the device (≤ 50, oldest dropped), sent one at a time, a failed/offline one retried (online, visible again, every 60 s); a refused one is dropped. Saved in `gameData('leaderboard')` (in the Arcade Backup Code): `{pid (24 × [a-z0-9]), grade, on (default true), queue, day, week, weekStars, endless, id (the device's 6-char id when the scoreboard returns one)}`. THE SCREEN: first open asks the grade (6/7/8, changeable in MY SETTINGS with the switch "Show me on the leaderboard" + "Only your avatar name and scores are shared. Never your real name."); grade switch (other grades viewable); tabs STARS THIS WEEK / MOST IMPROVED / PRACTICE STREAK / ENDLESS (+ game picker, `Leaderboard.ENDLESS_GAMES`); top 10 with #1–3 gold/silver/bronze, the device's own entry highlighted (`isMe`), else "You: 12 stars this week. Keep going!" from this device's numbers; "Resets every Monday · Updated a minute ago"; boards cached 60 s (sessionStorage); while a board loads: "Loading the leaderboard…" (`.ui-msg.loading` = the UI kit's spinner, `.lb-loading`), never the break message; a failed read with a saved board = that board + "Couldn't refresh — showing the board from 2 hours ago" (`.lb-stale`); nothing saved (or disabled) = "Leaderboard is taking a break. Your progress is still saved!". `?teacher` shows each entry's 6-character id (Mat blocks a player in the Sheet's Blocked tab) and under the board how the last request went (`.lb-diag`: "Last request failed: timeout after 25 s (took 50.1 s, tried twice)."). Tests: tests/leaderboard.spec.js (warm-up, simple requests, a slow board, the retry + saved board, the break message). NAMES ARE WORD NUMBERS: `Avatar.nameNumbers(av)` / `nameFromNumbers([t, a, n])` (unknown, retired or NEVER-USE = "Mystery"), `wordNumber`, `wordAt`; the numbers = each word's place (from 1) in avatar-names.js's APPEND-ONLY lists (a retired word is `'#Word'`: its number stays reserved); avatar-code.js's TABLE reads the same lists, so share codes use the same numbers (old codes decode unchanged). `Arcade.Leaderboard` (`available/settings/setGrade/setOn/canSend/board/mine/queue/flush`) and `Arcade.LeaderboardScreen.state()` for tests.
- THE LOCKER (`shared/locker.js` = `Arcade.LockerUI` + `shared/locker.css`; loaded by the floor page, and on demand anywhere else through `Arcade.Locker.open(…)` in avatar-badge.js, like the creator). Opened from: Select Player's player card (`#skinsBtn` "LOCKER" + "n of N" = items + this instrument's skins; Player 2's turn dresses the guest), THE AVATAR BADGE's menu on every top bar (LOCKER · 12 of 58, the badge's instrument), and a results screen's UNLOCKED! card (`Skins.announce` adds OPEN LOCKER next to WEAR IT); an overlay (z 85) that closes back to exactly where the student was (focus included), its keys never reach the game. NEW: `Arcade.Locker.fresh(member)` = items (and that instrument's skins, keys `skin:<id>@<member>`) earned since the Locker was last opened (`gameData('locker').seen`; the first time a device is asked, everything already earned counts as seen); the badge shows a pink dot + "2 new" in its menu, the Locker's tabs a dot and the tiles NEW; opening it clears the dot (`markOpened`, window `arcade:locker` redraws every badge; `Skins.announce` fires it too); nothing in ?demo&unlockall. `Arcade.Locker.count(member)`, `LockerUI.state()` for tests: tests/locker.spec.js. The Locker itself (`#locker`), tabs OUTFIT (tops, shoes, hair colors) · HATS (heads) · EXTRAS (the instrument's accessory skin, `hand` items, `back` items, name plates, eyes + mouths expressions) · PETS · BACKGROUNDS (a locked one = its own dimmed picture) · EFFECTS (avatar effects + the instrument's color skins) (`LK_TABS` in player.js; ←/→ between tabs). Avatar items are worn everywhere (`Avatar.set`; Player 2's turn dresses the guest), skins per instrument (`Skins.equip`). Locked = dark silhouette + lock + requirement + progress ("61 of 100 ★ so far").
- THE PRIZE COUNTER + THE SHARED WALLET: ONE WALLET, TWO COUNTERS. `shared/tokens.js` = `Arcade.Tokens`, loaded on EVERY page (shared/version.js writes its tag with the UI kit's; it reads nothing until called) and THE ONLY THING THAT READS OR WRITES THE WALLET: `tokens`, `converted` ({'m:<member>' | 'g:<game>': stars turned in}) and `convertedLeft`, stored where they always were, in the Arcade Quest save (`gameData('arcade-quest').save`), so old balances, QUEST CODES and Backup Codes keep working; a device that never opened Arcade Quest gets only `{wallet: true, tokens, converted}` there (no progress), which Arcade Quest's save.js `upgrade()` turns into a fresh save KEEPING them; Arcade Quest's NEW GAME (`Q.save.reset()`, also the Test Arena's reset) keeps the wallet too (tokens + `converted`), so no star is counted twice. API: `balance/add/spend/restore` (a save code), `starSources()` (the saved member's `allStars` + fixed-player games its groups don't cover, exactly as the booth did), `freshStars()`, `waiting()` (= fresh × RATE), `turnIn()` → {stars, tokens}, `catalog()` (every `unlock.shop` avatar item: `{key, field, id, name, full, questOnly}`; never event items or Band Ninja gear), `item/owned/price(key)` → {price, full, weekly}, `canBuy(key, {counter: 'prize' | 'quest'})` / `buy(...)` → {ok, why ('unknown'|'questOnly'|'owned'|'short'), text, need, price} (buying = `spend` + `store.ownItem`; wearing is the caller's), `weekly(date)`, `wish()/setWish(key)/wishProgress()`, `resultLine()`; every change fires window `arcade:tokens`. THE SETTINGS block at its top (Mat edits): `RATE` 5 tokens a star, `featuredDiscount` 0.2. Nothing can be counted twice: both counters use the same `converted` record, balance and `ownItem`. WHAT'S SOLD: every {shop} item at BOTH counters for the same price, EXCEPT THE MANOR COLLECTION = avatar-parts.js's `MANOR_COLLECTION` list at its end (Mat edits: Pirate hat 200, Magic wand 250, Fireflies Night 200, Royal crown 400), which sets `unlock.booth = 'quest'`: sold ONLY at Arcade Quest's booth; the Prize Counter shows them on their own shelf behind glass with a lock tag "Only at the Token Booth in Arcade Quest!" (a try-on, no BUY; `Tokens.buy` refuses with `why: 'questOnly'`); owners keep them. Requirements read "250 tokens at the Prize Counter" / "… at the Token Booth in Arcade Quest". CHARMS stay Arcade Quest only (the counter's foot says where they are). THE SEASONAL SHOP: NEW items with unlock `{shop: <price>, season: '<event id>'}` (avatar-parts.js's SEASONAL SHOP section + its one commented `SEASON_SHOP` table of ids, events and prices (Mat edits); 3 per event: Spooky Season Jack-o'-Lantern Treat Bucket 150 / Spiderweb Cape 250 / Black Cat 350; Winter Fest Hot Cocoa Mug 150 / Cozy Knit Sweater 250 / Snowy Owl 350; Friendship Week (only 8 days: cheaper) Heart Balloon 150 / Candy Heart name plate 150 / Teddy Bear 300; MIOSM Marching Shako 250 / Conductor's Tailcoat 300 / Music Note Sparkles 350 (avatar-fx.js `FX.notesparkles`, slow, still with reduced motion); Spring Bloom Rain Boots 150 / Polka-Dot Umbrella 200 / Ladybug 250; Summer Send-Off Ice Pop 100 / Hawaiian Shirt 250 / Hermit Crab 300; every id also at the END of its avatar-code.js TABLE list and of `QUEST_V5.cosmetics`): sold at BOTH counters ONLY while `Seasons.active()` is that event (so `?season=<id>` previews it), owned forever once bought; outside it `canBuy` says `why: 'offSeason'` ("The Black Cat is only sold during Spooky Season."); never the Prize of the Week (the weekly pool leaves them out), never discounted, never Manor Collection, never identity items. `Tokens.seasonal()` → {ev, daysLeft, left ("gone in 12 days!" / "gone tomorrow!" / "Last day!"), items}, `nextSeason()` → {ev, from, items}, `seasonOn(id)`. THE SEASONAL SHELF at the Prize Counter: during an event a decorated shelf at the top of the wall (`.pz-s-season`, the event's `colors` as `--ev1/--ev2`, a row of its `deco` emoji while the Seasonal look is on) with its sign "🎃 SPOOKY SEASON SHELF · gone in 12 days!", try-on and BUY like every prize, and a light touch on the rest of the counter (`.pz-touch`: a two-color garland along the glass case; still); between events no shelf but a small card "Coming soon: Winter Fest shelf, Dec 1" with the next event's 3 items as silhouettes (`.pz-soon`). Arcade Quest's booth lists them first during the event, tagged "SEASONAL: gone in 12 days!" (`.q-season`), and not at all otherwise. A seasonal WISH shows "gone in 12 days!" on the lobby's wish bar; if the event ends unbought, `wish()` clears it and the lobby says "The Black Cat will be back next Spooky Season." for 14 days (`wishGone()`, gone as soon as a new wish is set). The Locker / Create Your Player requirement (avatar.js): "Prize Counter: 350 tokens (Spooky Season only)" during the event, else "Returns to the Prize Counter next Spooky Season". The lobby's EVENT PANEL (season-lobby.js `shopLine`) adds "New on the Prize Counter: 3 Spooky Season prizes" under the ladder (opens the Prize Counter). Tests: tests/seasonal-shelf.spec.js (+ the smoke page "Prize Counter: seasonal shelf", `?season=spooky`, at iPad sizes and on a phone: a pages.js entry may give its own `sizes`). THE PRIZE OF THE WEEK (`weekly(date)` → {itemId, key, item, price, full, endsAt, daysLeft}): the same on every device, weeks from Monday (by calendar date, `store.today()`, so `?demo&today=` works), picked from the items sold at BOTH counters by the week's number: one seeded shuffle per round through the whole list (a round never starts with the last round's final prize), so every prize comes round before any repeats; price = round5(full × (1 − featuredDiscount)) at both counters.
