JOBS (`job()` per machine, shown on the panel and the voice box: rhythms with `RhythmStaff.engrave` + Mr. Graham's Counting, p / f bold italic, accents `>`): EXACT COUNTS (every showtime): at 0 a machine waits `exact.confirmMs` 350 (CONFIRM; the panel says "…and STOP" at 1); a hit then = an OVER-HIT (+`overhitAdd` 2, a sour glitch, "Too many! Count your hits and stop."), silence = reboot; then the next target listens only after `gapMs` 400 of silence. FREEZE (from 2): on a `freeze.chance` share of count machines Maestro Moose's hand (✋, `#stHand`) rises partway (`at`) for `freeze.beats` beats: every machine walks at `walkMul` .5, a hit = +`penalty` 2 ("Freeze means FREEZE!"), "GO!" after (visual); never within `guard` 2 s of the front. RHYTHM MACHINES (from 3): `rhythm.share` of machines ask for `rhythm.measures` measures of a rhythm from `SNARE_RHYTHMS` (a filter over Rhythm Dojo's 4/4 `RD_LEVELS` cells, growing by showtime: 3 quarters/eighths … 8 everything incl. syncopation; half of each draw from the showtime's new rhythms) at `rhythm.bpm` (× `nightmareBpm` 1.1), after a silent one-measure count-in (big beat numbers + dots); judged by `RhythmJudge` with `rhythm.window` ms (rests included: a hit in a rest or an extra fails); pass = the measure turns green, fail = a glitch + the same rhythm again with early/late/extra ticks on the staff. SOFT AND LOUD (from 5, needs THE SOUNDCHECK: 4 soft + 4 loud hits, the split halfway on a log scale, `gameData.snareDyn` `{soft, loud, split, at}`; a pair under `dyn.minRatio` apart = "Make the soft hits softer and the loud hits louder" and again; runs before the first showtime ≥ 5 and on "Redo soundcheck"): p machines count only soft hits (loud = +1, "Softer!"), f only loud ("Louder!", nothing counted); ACCENT machines (from 6): eighths from `accent.patterns`, accented hits loud, the rest soft ("Accents louder!" / "Keep the others soft!"). ROLLS: the Lurker's roll must be fast enough AND even (the gaps' CV ≤ `roll.maxCv` .35, NIGHTMARE .25; clumpy = fills × `clumpyMul` .3, "Smooth it out: even hits!"); NIGHTMARE's Glitch Lurker = a CRESCENDO roll (each third of the ring louder than the last by `crescK`; the ring shows a hairpin; no mid-roll glitch). THE TEMPO-LOCK MAESTRO (NIGHTMARE, showtime 8): each phase "Match my tempo!" at `maestro.bpm` (84/96/108): steady eighths for `beats` beats, a hit within `window` ms of the grid fills it, the average drift over the last beat drains it ("You're rushing!" / "You're dragging!"); on Normal the snare Maestro keeps its exact count. FAIRNESS: `fair()` counts every requirement (measures × beats, count-in, freeze beats, roll time) and slows a machine until its job fits in `fair.margin` of its walk. NEW DRUM CHALLENGE cards (`#specialCard.drum`, one sentence + a tiny animated example; `gameData.files['drum-*']`, the first time a device meets each; a card already open for a special machine gets it as one more line), the snare card lists what each showtime adds, the Malfunction Files have DRUM CHALLENGES (`#drumFilesSec`). Stars and progress keys unchanged. `?demo&snarejob=count|p|f|rhythm|accent|roll|tempo|freeze` forces a job; Space = a hit at the level the job wants. `Arcade.Showtime.snare()` / `snarePlan()` / `snareJob()` / `soundcheck()` / `timingCheck()` for tests: tests/showtime-snare.spec.js.
- Characters are original (characters.js: Tubby Tusk walrus/tuba, Professor Hoot owl/flute, Snapjaw Sal gator/snare, Rico Bandit raccoon/sax, Maestro Moose conductor). NEVER: bears, rabbits, chickens, foxes, a pizzeria, nights/clock hours 12–6 AM, security cameras, doors or power meters, or anyone trapped inside. They are just malfunctioning machines. Colors = `--anim-*` tokens.
- Progress per MEMBER (`byMember`): `setLevel(picker.state.progressKey, member.id, lv, …)`; snare = `'showtime-malfunction:count'` under `snare`. `gameData`: `{spooky: 'mild'|'spooky'|'jump', jumpDay, scareVisual, files: {<special id>: {seen, beaten}}, storySeen, diff: 'normal'|'extra', extraVisuals, extraSeen: {member: true}}`.
- DIFFICULTY: NORMAL | NIGHTMARE (`.diff-set` toggle, remembered). Normal = levels.js as written (never change it for NIGHTMARE); NIGHTMARE = each row's `x` column (`count`, `snare` ≤ 16, `speed` walk multiplier, `blurb`, `boss: {count, snare}`) merged by `rowFor(lv)`; same bots, lanes, atOnce, pool. Locked until THIS member has ≥ 1 ★ on The 5:00 Show on Normal in any mode (`?demo`: open); the first unlock shows `#resUnlock` + plays `nightmare-unlocked`. Saves under the Normal key + `':extra'` (`showtime-malfunction:extra`, `…:scale-Eb:extra`, `…:count:extra`); the picker reads them via `ModePicker.mount({keySuffix, max: 24})`. `allStars` counts every `<game>:…` key, so NIGHTMARE stars are in the totals (games.js `maxStars` 48 = 24 + 24). Difficulty and SPOOKY LEVEL are separate: the first switch to NIGHTMARE sets Spooky once; Mild still works and only changes visuals. `body.extra-mode` + `spooky-mode` = redder emergency light (`.emergency`, slow pulse), more cabinet static, eyes flicker more; Mild + EXTRA looks like Mild. Mild = glitch/static only; Spooky = darker + a silent lean-in at game over. Flicker always slow and gentle, never rapid or full-screen; reduced motion = none (and no twitch/lean-in).
- Sounds (sounds.js, screen `showtime-malfunction`): `showtime-start` (falls back to level-start), `attack-tick` (`echo: 60`, keep < 0.1 s so fast tonguing counts), `reboot`, `spotlight-out`, `showtime-over`, `nightmare-unlocked` (results, first unlock; falls back to `skin-unlocked`); results use the shared events. No music or ambience during play. Achievements: defeat Maestro Moose (level 8) → the `animatronic` skin; on NIGHTMARE → `nightmare` (Nightmare Animatronic: unlock rule `suffix: ':extra'`). Specials: `special-alert`, `special-intro`, `special-<id>` (8, fallback `special-alert`); Jump Scare: `scare-sting-1/-2/-3` (`cap: 3`). All unpitched noise.
- SPECIAL MACHINES (levels.js `SHOWTIME_SPECIALS`, one commented table: `chance` per showtime (0 in 1–2, .15 in 3–4, .25 in 5–7, .30 in 8), `nightmare` +.10, per machine `speed`/`count`/`snare`/`points` + its own settings; characters.js draws them, `SPECIAL_IDS`, tokens `--anim-tin`…`--anim-ollie`): an animatronic walking on (`specialize` in game.js, never the boss) may become one; it REPLACES that regular one (same note from `ModePicker.sequence`; extra notes for the Duet Dolls / the Jester / the minis come from the same result's `pool`), never more than one special on the floor at once (Split Sprocket's minis excepted). Turbo Tin (2× speed, 1–2 plays), Tuba Tank (0.6×, 2× plays, `.a-plate`s pop off per counted play), Long Tone Lurker (attacks don't count; the note held steadily fills a ring over `hold[lv]` s, draining at `drain` when it stops/changes; its "Hold it! One long, steady note." replaces the tongue hint; SNARE: a roll of ≥ `rollRate` hits/s for `roll[lv]` s), Duet Dolls (two different notes in turns A, B, A…; the other doll's note does nothing; snare = a normal count), Glitch Jester (after `switchAt` of its plays its note changes, a slow 0.7 s skew/fade; snare = normal), Split Sprocket (rebooted = two minis in the adjacent lanes, 1.5× speed, 1 play each; `G.total` += 2), Blackout Bot (voice box + panel dark until z ≥ `revealAt`, then a fade, no flicker), Oil Can Ollie (every `every` s +1 play on the nearest other walking machine, max `maxAdd` each, oil-drop icons; not the Lurker). A special's FIRST appearance on a device (`gameData.files[id].seen`) pauses the showtime (`G.paused`: the band, `G.t` and scares stop; attacks ignored) for the "NEW MALFUNCTION DETECTED!" card (`#specialCard`, `special-alert` + `special-intro`); the card (`.overlay.special-ov`, z-index 700, the arena `isolation: isolate`) is always above every animatronic; later ones play `special-<id>` (fallback `special-alert`). Rebooting one = `points.special` + its `points` (score only: stars are unchanged) and `files[id].beaten++`. The MALFUNCTION FILES (`#files`, the level screen's button with "n/8"): a silhouette until met, "Spotted!" once met, the whole card (how to beat it, times rebooted) once rebooted; saved in gameData (so in the Arcade Backup Code). `?demo&special=<id or part of it>` makes every animatronic that may be special that one, from Showtime 1 (tests). HYBRIDS (`hybrid: [A, B]` entries in `SHOWTIME_SPECIALS.machines`, ids never renamed): Glitch Lurker (hold; halfway the note glitches: switch and keep holding, the ring keeps its fill; snare: the roll speeds up from 6 to 8 hits a second halfway), Blackout Jester (dark until 40 %, then a glitch; snare: a count), Sprocket Dolls (a duet pair that splits into two minis, each keeping one doll's note; snare: two 1-hit minis), Oil Tank (1.5× plays, 4 plates popped by its first plays; it oils the others only while a plate is left), Turbo Blackout (fast, 1–2 plays, dark until a third of the way), Duet Tank (two notes in turns, 1.5× plays, a plate per play). A special's `traits` = its parents (a plain special: its own id): every trick's code asks `has(b, '<parent>')`, `get(id, parent, key)` = the hybrid's own setting else the parent's, so a hybrid runs both parents' code paths. `hybrids` in levels.js: `from` 5, `share` by showtime (0.3 at 5–6, 0.4 at 7–8) of the special chance, NIGHTMARE too; one special on the floor at a time, so never two hybrids. THE FAIRNESS CHECK (`fair()`, `hybrids.fair`): a hybrid is slowed until its plays ÷ `rate` (snare `snareRate`) + its hold + `glitch` fit in `margin` of the time its note shows. Drawn from both parents (characters.js HYBRIDS: A's head on B's body, both felts, `.a-stitch` seams, an odd right eye from the other parent, the trick's parts); the card adds "HYBRID!" + both parents' names (`#spHybrid`); the Malfunction Files have a HYBRIDS section ("n/14"); a hybrid's walk-on sound is its first parent's `special-<id>`. `Arcade.Showtime.choose()` / `specialize(spec)` for tests (tests/showtime-hybrids.spec.js).
- JUMP SCARE (the third SPOOKY LEVEL, `data-spooky="jump"`): hidden unless `shared/teacher-settings.js` `Arcade.TEACHER.JUMP_SCARE_ALLOWED` (a device that had it plays Spooky); turning it on ALWAYS asks first (`#jumpWarn`: "This mode has sudden jump scares with loud sounds. Are you sure?" YES / NO + "Visual scares only (no scare sounds)" = `gameData.scareVisual`); never the default; `jumpDay` = the day it was turned on: on any other day (`store.today()`) it is back to Spooky (`drawSpooky`, at load, on the hub and at each showtime start). It has every Spooky visual (`body.spooky-mode` + `jump-mode`) + 1 scare a showtime, 2 on the longer ones (≥ `longFrom` 7 animatronics, or the boss) (levels.js `SHOWTIME_SCARES`: planned by PROGRESS, not seconds: `at` = the share of the show's animatronics that have walked on (short [.4], long [.25, .55]; `planScares`/`armScares`, fired `delay` 1 s after), so a quick player gets them too; never before `notBefore` 6 s, ≥ `apart` 6 s apart, postponed while the Maestro is within `bossGuard` 5 s of the front or has ≤ 2 plays left in a phase; skipped at the very end of a show (nothing left to come and the last ones near the front)). The Settings panel says "Jump Scare resets to Spooky each new day." under the spooky buttons (only when it's allowed). A scare (`scare()`, `#scare` over the arena, ~1.3 s + a 0.7 s beat): LUNGE (dark, a machine rushes out), EYES (lights cut to black, two eyes, then the lunge), POPUP (a head from the bottom edge), BAND (the stage band's heads snap toward you, the stage leans in; only with someone on the stage); reduced motion = FADE (darkness + eyes, nothing moves). During it `G.scare` freezes everything, `Pitch.suppress` ignores the mic, it never costs a spotlight. Only darkness, motion and scale: no bright or white flash, no strobe, no shake (measured: at most one light change per spot per scare). Sound: one of `scare-sting-1/-2/-3` (unless visual only), sounds.js `cap: 3` = THE LOUDNESS CAP (sfx.js: peak and loudest 50 ms never > 3 dB over the arcade's normal loudest effect, a file scaled + a hard limiter; verified with a deliberately over-loud file: +3.0 dB). `?demo&scare=lunge|eyes|popup|band` (with Jump Scare on) = that scare 3 s in; `Arcade.Showtime.scare(kind)` for tests.
- Cabinet: `shape: 'showtime'` (crooked top, crack, wire, tape, a missing button), `marquee: 'showtime'` (MALFUNCTION flickers slowly, the F bulb is dead), `screen: 'showtime'` (static + red eyes); 3D profile `showtime` with a `lamp` topper.

## Sustain Speedway (`sustain-speedway/`)
- THE UI KIT: a kit pause during the countdown restarts it from 3 on RESUME (like a hidden tab); results are wide, levels button `#resLevels`, `actsFirst`: place + car, stars, title, message, tiles → THE BUTTONS (NEXT TRACK yellow + focused, else TRY AGAIN; TRY AGAIN, TRACKS, the plain GARAGE) → NEW IN THE GARAGE! (its filled GARAGE; then no plain one) → "Race details" (`#resMore`, a small heading: the teacher ghost line, the longest hold, "Your tuning", the difficulty, the per-lap chart + lap notes). The buttons are on screen without scrolling on a phone in portrait, an iPad both ways and a laptop (`.ui-acts-first` shrinks the place, the car and the stars on short screens).
- Long-tone and intonation racing game, LISTENS (pitch.js + mic-gate.js + sfx.js). The car moves only while the lap's target note sounds (`reading.pc === item.pc`, any octave; cents are measured from the TARGET with `reading.midi`, not `reading.cents`, because the engine's hysteresis can hold a pitch class up to ±65 cents away). Speed = `SPEEDWAY_RULES.weights` × (intonation credit: 1 within the difficulty's `tol`, sliding to `low.credit` at `low.cents`) + (pitch steadiness: std of cents over `windowMs`) + (volume steadiness: std of dB). Wrong note (`wrongFrames` readings in a row) = hard `brake` + "Target: B♭" flash; silence = `coast`. NITRO: within `nitro.cents` and both steadiness scores ≥ `nitro.steady` for `holdMs` → ×`boost` while it lasts ("IN THE ZONE!"). Difficulties Rookie/Pro/Virtuoso (tol 20/12/6), remembered in gameData `diff`. The UI says IN TUNE and STEADY only; never claim to measure tone quality.
- `levels.js`: `SPEEDWAY_TRACKS` (8: name, blurb, laps, lap (s at full speed; `[first, last]` = growing), pool, scene, sky, tunnels, rivals {name, color token, pace}) + `SPEEDWAY_RULES`. Notes ONLY from `ModePicker.sequence` (count = laps). A lap = a distance (full-speed seconds). PIT STOP between laps (`pitSec`, breathing coach, next note shown); rivals pit too (`paceProgress`). Race clock stops while `Pitch.isSuppressed`. Stars: 1st 3, 2nd 2, 3rd 1, last 0; winning (3 ★ on the previous track) opens the next; `?demo` opens all.
- Progress per MEMBER (`byMember`): `setLevel(picker.state.progressKey, member.id, track, {stars, best: finish time in tenths})`. `gameData('sustain-speedway')` = `{diff, ghosts: {'<progressKey>|<member>|<track>': {t, p: [progress every ghostEvery s]}}, bestLap: {same key: s}, achievements: {'virtuoso-win': true, 'perfect-lap': true}, wins, garage: {body, paint, decal, number}, garageSeen: ['body:muscle', …]}`. Ghost = best total time.
- The road is a canvas (requestAnimationFrame, dpr ≤ 1.5, simple polygons/gradients, colors from `--sw-*` tokens); the HUD is HTML/SVG (tuning speedometer, note card on a light `--screen`, STEADY meter). Reduced motion: no speed lines, no sway, no pulses. THE CARS (`sustain-speedway/cars.js`, `Arcade.SpeedwayCars`): 2D, from behind, each drawn ONCE per LOOK (body, paint, FINISH, two-tone roof, decal + its color, number, rims, spoiler, plate text) × size bucket × sky × brake state into a cached SPRITE (gradient body, shoulder highlight, the sky's rim light, valance, soft shadow, tires with tread and the rim style's edge, diffuser, exhausts, plate, tail lights with a soft halo), then only copied each frame; per frame only the underglow (a cached glow sprite; a flat ellipse in LITE), the Dune Buggy's bounce, the hovers' shimmer and the nitro flames in the chosen color (none with reduced motion). BODIES (17): coupe, mini, muscle, wagon, buggy, openwheel, hover, maestro (rival only) + pickup, rally (mud flaps, roof vent, light pod), stock (number panels, blade spoiler), hotrod (blower + stacks over the roof), kart (driver on show), supercar, monster (huge tires, one lane wide), retrohover, bumper (round, a pole). FINISHES (`fillPaint`): gloss, matte (soft highlight), metallic (a baked flake pattern), pearl (a sheen toward the track's light), chrome (the sky reflected), neon (glowing edges). DARK PAINTS (luminance < `DARK` .16) get a light RIM around the whole sprite (`--sw-rim-light`) + a brighter highlight on every track. SPOILERS: 'stock' = the body's own (`OWN_SPOILER`), else none / lip / wing / ducktail / tall on the body's `DECK`. `Cars.side(ctx, x, y, w, o)` = THE SIDE VIEW from `SIDE` profiles (rims drawn: classic, five-star, disc, whitewall, turbine, off-road, gold, neon rim), `rimURL(style)`, `sideHeight(body)`. THE GARAGE (`sustain-speedway/garage.js`, `Arcade.SpeedwayGarage`; the GARAGE button just left of START in the track select's sticky row (`LevelSelect.show({beside})`: a secondary button as tall as START, its car icon + "new" dot, icon-only with aria-label "Garage" at ≤ 560 px), never the arcade's menus): tabs BODY · PAINT (color (~26: the neon tokens, Gloss Black free, Matte Black (always matte), silver, gold, teal, navy…: `--sw-paint-*`), FINISH, TWO-TONE roof) · DECALS (16: stripes single / double / offset, flames, stars, checker, lightning, an original swoosh, music notes, treble clef, number 1–99, camo, polka dots, tiger stripes, gradient fade; + DECAL COLOR = any unlocked paint or its own colors) · PARTS (wheels, spoiler, underglow, nitro flame color incl. a slow rainbow, trail: none / streak / sparkles / notes / stars, drawn at speed in game.js, never in LITE or with reduced motion) · PLATE (a preset word from `plateWords` or 4 digit steppers: NO typed text anywhere); the preview = the rear view on a turntable with a gentle sway, or the SIDE VIEW (`#gView`); RANDOMIZE (`randomize()`: unlocked items only); 3 SAVED LOADOUTS (`gameData.loadouts` [{name, car}]: the name is tapped through `loadoutNames` (Car 1/2/3, Race Day, Showtime, Cruiser), SAVE HERE / WEAR); ←/→ between tabs, Tab stays inside, Esc closes. Saved per device in `gameData.garage` `{body, paint, finish, paint2, decal, dcolor, number, rims, spoiler, glow, flame, trail, plate}` (`clean()` puts a locked or unknown choice back to the default); an old save = Coupe + the instrument's color with decal AUTO (null: stripes when the equipped skin has them, i.e. Racing Stripes). THE UNLOCKS = `SPEEDWAY_GARAGE` in levels.js (Mat edits; rules `{free}`, `{stars}` (Speedway stars, device-wide), `{tracks}` finished, `{wins}` different tracks won, `{winCount}` races won (`gameData.wins`, seeded from old wins), `{track: n}`, `{achievement}` ('virtuoso-win', 'perfect-lap' = a lap averaging within `nitro.cents`, 'teacher-ghost'), `{bestLap}`, `{cleanLaps}` (`gameData.cleanLaps`: in the zone ≥ `cleanLapShare` of a lap after the nitro wait), `{breath}` (the best steady hold, any instrument), `{dynZones}` (`gameData.dynNailed`: the right dynamic ≥ `dynNailShare` of a zone), `{slurs}` (`gameData.slurSmooth`), `{arcadeStars}` (every game), `{any}`), checked from saved progress so old progress counts; locked = a dark silhouette + how to earn it + progress ("2 of 3 clean laps so far"), never pickable; a locked saved choice falls back. THE NEXT UNLOCK (`nextUnlock()`: the locked item closest to being earned) shows on the track select (`#garageNext`). NEW IN THE GARAGE!: `gameData.garageSeen`; the results screen shows the EARNED ones since (free items are never announced) (a card, amber centered heading, with a FILLED amber GARAGE button that breathes twice, `.garage-fill`) and the hub's button a "new" dot. EVERY results screen has exactly ONE GARAGE: the card's when something is new, else an outline one (the track menu's look + car icon, `#resGarage`, the kit's `more`) after TRACKS, NEXT still the yellow one; the garage opens over the results and closes back to them. The chosen car races, the ghost is the same car see-through, and the results show it. `?demo&unlockall` opens everything. Tests: tests/speedway-garage.spec.js + tests/speedway-garage-deep.spec.js. Cars and cabinet are original. NO TWO CARS OVERLAP ON SCREEN, AND NOTHING GLITCHES (`placeCars`, drawing only: progress, positions, times and standings never change): three lanes, yours the middle; the others keep to a side lane (rivals left, right, left; the ghost right). THE STARTING GRID: before GO and for the first `GRID.hold` s every rival sits in a fixed slot (2 a row), then the grid offset fades over `GRID.blend` s so the cars leave it smoothly; the ghost joins once the grid is gone. STABLE LANES: a car changes lane only after `LANE_BLOCK_MS` (400) blocked in a row, then stays `LANE_STAY_MS` (1.5 s); moves slide at `LANE_SPEED`; cars are placed in the order they were drawn last frame (ties by index), so nothing flips. NO JUMPS: a car that must be held back (never drawn over another car or yours) eases its drawn distance there and back (`EASE`, at most `EASE_MAX` road units a second). Reduced motion: no sliding, still at most one lane change per 1.5 s. `G.carsDrawn` (name, idx, lane, grid, d, drawnD, off, box) for tests: tests/speedway-lanes.spec.js.
- THE COUNTDOWN: `A.countdown` (shared/countdown.js), CLASSIC 3·2·1 one second apart + a spoken GO, voice prefix 'race' (`race-count-3/-2/-1/-go`, mic: true because the mic is already listening; missing = the Dojo Duel voice, then the tick), on the race's own countdown clock (`G.cd`, `countTick` in the loop, only while the page is visible). Each number shows as its clip plays. The countdown's sounds are `Sfx.prefer`red when the track screen shows and `whenReady` (≤ 0.8 s) before the first beat. THE RACE STARTS WHEN THE MIC IS LIVE: after the GO voice (`G.goHeard`) the loop waits until `Pitch.isSuppressed()` is over, then shows GO!, starts the car, the clock and scoring (`G.liveAt`); a note started during GO counts from then on. Hiding the page during the countdown stops it (`Sfx.hush`); it restarts from 3 when the page comes back (`G.restarts`). `race-countdown` (the old one-file countdown) is no longer used.
- PITCH STEERING (drawing only: `driveStep(dt)` = the speed from what is heard, never from the steering): while the lap's note sounds the car's sideways place shows the pitch (`steerOf(cents)`): within the difficulty's `tol` = the center line, beyond it FLAT drifts LEFT, SHARP drifts RIGHT, reaching the road's edge at `RULES.steer.edge` 50¢; smoothed `smoothMs` 250, back to the center over `backMs` when the note stops (breath, pit, a wrong note: wrong notes still brake and never steer). Past `rumble` .75: the red-white rumble strip on that side glows (a soft 2 Hz pulse), the car wobbles a little (none under reduced motion) and `#steerWord` shows "FLAT ◀" / "▶ SHARP". Rivals/the ghost avoid the drifting car's box (placeCars as before); the ghost replays its own steering (`ghosts[key].s`, one sample per `ghostEvery`; older ghosts drive straight). The tuning meter's ends say "◀ flat" / "sharp ▶". One-time hint at the first GO (`gameData.steerHint`, `#steerHint`). THE INTONATION REPORT ("Your tuning", `#resTuning` on the results, above the per-lap chart): per target note (written + octave, e.g. "D4": `noteKey`), only holds ≥ `RULES.report.reportHoldSec` 1 s count (`holdReading`/`endHold`): average signed cents, wobble (the holds' std), zone time, the longest run within `tol`; a centered bar (flat left, sharp right, the in-tune band shaded) + "right on!" / "a little high (sharp)" / "a little low (flat)"; up to `maxTips` 2 TIPS from `sustain-speedway/tips.js` (`SPEEDWAY_TIPS`, Mat edits: `general` by member then family for an overall ≥ `tendency` 10¢ lean, note-specific `notes` (member + written notes + dir), else the `air` tip; `dynamics` tips for DYNAMICS ZONES); every note in tune = "Right on pitch! Great ears."; history per member `gameData.tuning[member]` = the last 10 races `[{d, n: {note: cents}}]` → a trend per note ("▲ getting closer!"). `?demo` D = drifting sharp (+25 → +45¢ over 4 s), F = drifting flat. `Arcade.Speedway.steer()` / `tipsFor` / `driveStep` for tests: tests/speedway-steering.spec.js (+ tests/speedway-helpers.js).
- THE LOOK OF A RACE (`sustain-speedway/scenery.js` = `Arcade.SpeedwayScene` + game.js `render`): each track's TIME OF DAY (levels.js `time`: sunset / golden / dusk / dawn / noon / night / neon; `TIMES` = sky tokens, sun or moon, stars, streetlights, ground, the cars' rim light; `sky` stays the fallback) + optional `weather` ('mist' | 'haze' | 'rain'). LAYERS: the sky (cached per size + time, slides with the bends), FAR and MID scenery canvases (`buildScenery`: drawn once per size, parallax with the bends; the Grand Prix's MID = grandstands of fans + banners), and the NEAR layer = roadside sprites every `PROP_GAP` road units (`SCENE_PROPS`: streetlights with light pools when the time has lights, palms, pines, cacti, rocks, buoys, grandstands, banners; each sprite drawn once at 96 px). SPEED FEEL (scaled by the speed): edge speed lines, rumble-strip ridges, tire smoke at GO and while braking; NITRO = a light shake + long soft streaks (no canvas self-redraw: too slow on old iPads); none of the shake/streaks/speed lines with reduced motion or Motion off. THE FINISH: a checkered gate + line on the road on the last lap (6 road units per second), the checkered flag waving as you cross (`FINISH.flagMs` 1.5 s; still under reduced motion), confetti for 1st / sparkles for 2nd–3rd, then THE PODIUM (`#podium`, `FINISH.podiumMs` 2.2 s; a tap, Enter, Space or Esc skips it): steps 2-1-3 with cars (`Cars.thumbURL`), your avatar (`avatarHTML`) + car on your step (4th = beside the podium), then the results; no new sound (race-finish, then the results' podium). SEASONAL TOUCHES in the race (the one game page that follows the seasonal look, on request): `SEASON_FX` by `Seasons.look()` (spooky: jack-o'-lanterns, bats, fog; winter: string lights, snowy pines, snow; frost: snow; spring: flowers, petals; friendship: hearts; miosm/concert: notes; harvest: hay, leaves; school: pennants), off with the Seasonal look switch unless `?season=` previews. GRAPHICS (the Settings panel's "Graphics: Auto / Full / Lite", `gameData.gfx`, default Auto): AUTO averages the frame time `PERF.from`–`PERF.to` s into a race and, over `PERF.ms` 22 ms, switches to LITE for good on the device (`gameData.gfxSlow`), saying nothing. LITE = sky + far layer only, no roadside things, no particles/weather/fog, no shake/streaks, fewer speed lines. Measured at 4× CPU throttle (headless): Full ≈ 36 ms, Lite ≈ 32 ms a frame. `Arcade.Speedway.fx()` / `podium()` for tests: tests/speedway-visuals.spec.js.
- DYNAMICS ZONES (levels.js `dyn: {zones, len, kinds}` from track 3; `RULES.dyn`): per lap `zones` stretches of `len` of the lap (none on slur laps; markings in turn from `kinds`, a random start per race: `makeZones`), each a colored band on the road + a big roadside sign (p / f in bold italic serif, the cresc. / decresc. hairpins; `roadSign`) and the `#dynBadge` (the marking + "softer!" / "louder!"). THE VOLUME CHECK (`volumeCheck`, `#vcheck` in the stage: "Play your note SOFT… now LOUD", `checkSec` 3 s each; RETRY; "I didn't hear…" = no LET'S RACE; too close = "Try a bigger difference", CONTINUE then uses `fallbackSpread`) runs before the countdown of the first dynamics race of a PLAY SESSION (`Arcade.session` 'sw-dyn' + sessionStorage `bandarcade.sw-dyn` {who, soft, loud} dB); tracks without `dyn` never ask; the Settings panel's "Volume check: Redo" clears it. In a zone the loudness (dB of the reading's level, smoothed `smoothMs`, as 0 = soft … 1 = loud) must be p ≤ `softMax`, f ≥ `loudMin`, cresc / decresc within `rampWindow` of a rising / falling ramp; wrong for `graceMs` = speed × `slow` .8 (S.dynMul in driveStep). Right dynamics = full speed, so best times stay comparable (the tracks keep their numbers: no new track versions). SLUR LAPS (levels.js `slurLaps` from track 5; `RULES.slur`): the target switches halfway (`slurTick`: "SLUR TO F!", the note card shows both notes with a slur arc, `slurStaff`; the pit card too); the old note still counts for `graceMs`; a silence of `gapMs` or more around the switch = a BREAK (speed × `slow` for `slowMs`, "Slur it!"), else "Smooth!". The second note (`slurPartner`) comes from the NOTES pool only: brass = the same primary fingering (shared/fingerings.js, now loaded here) when the pool has one, else 1–5 half steps; woodwinds 1–5 half steps (2–4 preferred) never across the break (clarinets B♭4|B4, saxes C♯5|D5). THE REPORT adds "Pitch in soft / loud zones" (≥ 20 readings each; soft = p and the soft ends of the hairpins) with tips.js `dynamics` tips (softFlat, loudSharp…) and "Slurs: 3 of 4 smooth". `?demo`: Space plays each zone's dynamic by itself, S = soft, L = loud, B = like Space with a 300 ms break at the switch (shared/pitch.js `Pitch.demoLevel` = the demo note's loudness). `Arcade.Speedway.dyn()` / `slur()` / `skipChecks()` for tests: tests/speedway-dynamics.spec.js.
- BREATH STATS (`RULES.breath`): the longest STEADY HOLD = unbroken tone within the difficulty's tolerance (each hold's in-tune run, `holdReading`). Results: "Longest steady hold: 7.4 s (new record!)" (`#resBreath`) + a gentle "🌬️ Breath record!" (`#brBadge`) when a saved record is beaten. The track select's PERSONAL RECORDS panel (`#records`, this instrument): the longest hold, a small bar chart of the last 10 races + a trend word, and each written note's best. Saved in `gameData.breath[member]` = `{best, notes: {<note>: {s, m}}, hist: [{d, best}]}`. THE TEACHER GHOST CHALLENGE (`sustain-speedway/teacher-ghosts.js`, Mat edits: `SPEEDWAY_TEACHER_GHOSTS = [{name, week, code}]`, how-to at its top; empty = no challenge): with `?teacher` the results offer SAVE AS TEACHER GHOST (`#resTeacherGhost`) = a UI.confirm with a read-only copy box (a ready line `{name: "Mr. Graham", week, code}` + COPY). THE CODE `SSG1-<payload>-<check>`: payload `1~track~notes~order~difficulty~total tenths~progress` (ghost samples every `ghostEvery` as thousandths of a lap, steps in base 36 with `step*count` runs), check = FNV-1a base 36; `decodeGhost` refuses a bad checksum, an unknown track/difficulty, or a run that doesn't finish its track (ignored; a console warning in ?demo). The newest `week` per track wins. A challenged track's card shows BEAT MR. GRAHAM (`.tg-badge`); racing it adds a gold see-through ghost (open-wheel, `--sw-teacher-gold`) with the name tag over it (joins after the grid, like your own ghost); the results say "You beat Mr. Graham's ghost by 2.3 s!" / "Mr. Graham's ghost won by 1.1 s: try again!" (`#resTeacher`); beating it sets `achievements['teacher-ghost']` = the garage paint Teacher's Gold (`sw-teacher-gold`). Nothing leaves the device. `Arcade.Speedway.breath()` / `ghostCode()` / `decodeGhost()` / `teacher(lv)` for tests: tests/speedway-breath-teacher.spec.js.
- SOUND: nothing during a race. The countdown before GO, `pit-in` in the pit (a rest), `race-finish`, then results `podium`/`star-earned`/`new-best-lap`. Skins: The Grand Prix won (level 8, 3 ★) → `stripes`; any win on Virtuoso → `helmet`.
- Bells/snare: blocked (games.js `noPlay.block`). `?demo`: hold Space = in tune, D = drifting sharp, W = wrong note, E = centered but wobbly; release = breathing. `Arcade.Speedway.debug()` for tests.
- Cabinet: `shape: 'speedway'` (a sit-down racer: SHAPES `wheel: [x, y, r]` draws a steering wheel instead of the joystick), `marquee: 'speedway'`, `screen: 'speedway'`; 3D profile `speedway` (`wheel: true`, topper `wing`).

## Blocktave (`blocktave/`)
- A side-view block-building survival game (zone adventure; `byMember`, `unpitched`, `noInstrument` for its TOUCH mode, `maxStars` 15) where PLAYING YOUR INSTRUMENT IS HOW YOU MINE AND CRAFT. Files: `rules.js` = `BT_RULES`, EVERY tuning number in one commented block (Mat tunes there, never in game.js); `recipes.js` = `BT_ITEMS` (item ids: never rename), `BT_RECIPES` (one commented table: ingredients IN ORDER, performance, output, count, `bench`) and `BT_CHAPTERS` (5 chapters × 3 milestone ids: never reorder or rename); `world.js` = `Arcade.BlocktaveWorld` (the `BLOCKS` table: block ids are saved, never renumber; the seeded generator; the saved format; `zone`, `light`, `room`); `challenges.js` = `Arcade.BlocktaveCard` (the challenge card); `game.js` (everything else). It loads `../rhythm-dojo/levels.js` for Rhythm Dojo's vetted 4/4 cells (Rhythm Rock, the snare's scale veins), never copied.
- ORIGINALITY (hard rule): the genre is fine, copying is not. No Minecraft names or terms (no creeper, steve, ender, nether, redstone, "craft" in a name, "crafting table"), no look-alike grass/dirt texture (the ground is plum "Glow Moss" with a neon sound-wave edge), no pickaxe (MALLETS and a baton), no 3×3 grid (THE MEASURE), no blocky pixel font (titles GN Neon / Bungee, text Atkinson), no borrowed sounds. Our own look: a neon, stage-lit block world, `--bt-*` tokens in theme.css; blocks drawn once per size into an atlas (game.js `drawTile`). The player is the student's own AVATAR (`Avatar.sprites(member)`, shared/instrument-sprites.js). Creatures are original and silly, never scary (Showtime Malfunction is the spooky game).
- THE WORLD: `world.w × world.h` (256 × 96) tiles from a SEED (`?seed=` picks a new world's seed; tests use it): surface, hills, caves, water; three biomes left to right (`biomes`: Reed Marsh = reed cane, cork, pad felt, maple; Brass Mountains = Brass Ore, Spring Veins, one peak always reaching the Treble Peaks; Percussion Canyon = maple, rawhide, Rhythm Rock, clay). LAYERS (`BW.zone`): rows above `peaksY` = TREBLE PEAKS (treble clef), rows from `deepY` = BASS DEPTHS (bass clef), the local surface + `shallow` = surface, else middle; the CLEF there is the treble/bass one, everywhere else the student's own (reading the other clef is part of the adventure; its notes are filtered to that clef's comfortable range, and the first `otherClefNames` cards show the name small under the staff). NOTE POOLS by layer, only from `buildSequence` for the member: surface `first5`, middle a concert scale, Peaks/Depths `chrom`. Drawn on ONE canvas: the parallax backdrop (below), only visible tiles, the light as one pixel per tile scaled up (LIGHT below), ≤ `fps` 60, dpr ≤ `dprMax`, nothing while hidden (rAF) or paused. DAY `dayS` 360 / NIGHT `nightS` 180 (the sky, the light and the music change: `blocktave-day` / `blocktave-night` loops, mic: false). Creatures come out only in the dark: at night above ground, in unlit caves any time. THE STARTER GUARANTEE (world.js `starter` / `ensureStarter`, rules.js `starterRange` 16 + `starter`): every world has, within `starterRange` columns of the spawn, ≥ 2 Maple and ≥ 2 Cork trees on dry ground (a reed in the way is cleared, never planted in water) and ≥ 12 Tone Ore at most `oreDepth` 8 rows under the ground; a new world's spawn is a dry column with at least `starterRange` dry columns around it; marsh trees `corkChance` / `mapleChance`. Each world saves its generator version `gen` (old saves = 1) and `repaired`: THE ONE-TIME REPAIR (`BW.repair`, on load) regenerates the seed's ORIGINAL with that `gen` and, if the starter check fails, grows the missing trees and ore ONLY into tiles still equal to the original (never over or inside anything placed or dug), then the toast "New trees have grown near your camp!" (tests: 500 seeds, chapter 1 end to end on 20 seeds, the repair). The save also keeps the Band Lockers' contents (`lockers`).
- MINING = PLAYING: a tap/click on a block within `player.reach` (MINE mode; BUILD mode places the hotbar's block, or USES a door/cot/locker/composer/podium/bench). Tap blocks (dirt, sand, leaves, plants, trunks, placed blocks) break at once (slate and bricks need a Wooden Mallet); MUSIC BLOCKS open a CHALLENGE CARD beside the block (it follows the camera) while the world runs at `challengeSlow` 0.3×: Tone/Brass Ore = the written note(s) (INSTRUMENT `Pitch.onHeld` pc; TOUCH the answer pad, the name as shown, key signature included), Scale/Spring Veins = 5–8 notes of a scale in order, Sustain Stone = one steady in-tune note (`sustainS`, cents from `reading.midi`; TOUCH: "Which key signature is this?" / "Name this scale" from scales.js), Rhythm Rock = a one-measure rhythm (a SILENT count-in light: nothing plays while listening; INSTRUMENT shared/onsets.js, TOUCH the TAP pad or Space; judged by shared/rhythm-judge.js minus `Calibration.lag`), Rest Crystal = rests (silent!) then the downbeat. Notes per card = `notes[kind][tool tier]` (hands, Wooden / Brass / Silver Mallet, Golden Baton: the tier sets what can be mined; the Brass Mallet makes Tone Ore one note; the Baton shortens long tones and rolls). INSTRUMENT mode drops `instrumentBonus` 2× what TOUCH drops. A wrong answer shakes the card and the block stays; `hintAfterWrong` 3 wrong in a row show the names. SNARE DRUM: Tone Ore = "play exactly N hits, then stop" (`snareCount`), Scale Veins / Rhythm Rock = rhythms, Sustain Stone / Sour Wisps = an EVEN ROLL (`rollS`, `rollRate`, `rollMaxCv`); every block is mineable, in both modes. THE MODE (INSTRUMENT | TOUCH: the title screen, the pause menu's "Switch to…", the Settings panel; `gameData.mode`, default INSTRUMENT) needs a saved instrument (requireInstrument). THE MICROPHONE LISTENS ONLY WHILE A CARD IS OPEN OR A CREATURE IS NEAR (INSTRUMENT mode; `listenSync`: `Pitch.pauseListening`), so the day/night music plays otherwise; every effect is unpitched noise and short.
- CRAFTING = PERFORMING (CRAFT / C, or a Luthier's Bench): THE MEASURE = a CRAFTING STATION (game.js `drawCraft`, `.bt-bench` in style.css, wood tokens `--bt-wood*` / `--bt-slot-bg`): a wooden workbench with a short staff carved into its top edge (decoration), 4 big labeled SLOTS in a row (`#measure`: "1st"…"4th" above, "Material 1"… under an empty one; ≥ 64 px; empty = dashed with a faint "+", filled = the item's big icon + name + a small ✕ that takes it out, the rest move up), an arrow → the RESULT box (`#recipeLine`: "?" until the slots match a recipe, then its icon, name, "× 4" and the performance: `PERF` / `PERF_TOUCH` / `PERF_SNARE`, e.g. "Play 1 note", "Concert B♭ scale", "4 steady beats"; "Needs a Luthier's Bench nearby" when a `bench: true` recipe has none within `benchRange`), and MAKE IT (`#perform`, yellow, DISABLED until a recipe matches and its bench is near) = the performance card: pass = the item is made, fail = the ingredients stay. Below: YOUR MATERIALS (`#craftItems`, item tiles: tap, or DRAG one onto the bench, = the next empty slot; the tiles block the browser's own image drag). Ingredients go IN ORDER (the same items in another order are no recipe: `BT_RECIPES` + `match()` are unchanged, only the screen is the station). ≤ 760 px: the slots 2 × 2, the result below. THE RECIPE BOOK (the RECIPE BOOK button): a recipe is FOUND the first time you hold all its ingredients (`gameData.found`); unknown ones are "?" silhouettes (`BT_ALWAYS_SHOWN` always show); tapping one fills the slots in order when you have its items, else shows what's missing in red ("Missing: 1 Maple", `fromBook`). The first bench is made by hand.
- BUILDING: Maple Planks, Soundproof Panel, Band Hall Brick, Stage Floor, Riser Steps (climb with JUMP), a 2-tall Door (BUILD mode taps open/close it), Glass, Stage Lamp (lights `light.lampRadius`: nothing spawns in light), Practice Cot (BUILD-tap: your wake-up spot, and at night sleep till morning when no creature is within `cotSafe`), Band Locker (`lockerSlots` stacks), Snack Bag (+`snackHeal` hearts: tap its hotbar slot twice), Music Stand, Metronome (creatures within its radius move at `metronomeSlow`), Tuner (Sour Wisps can't enter its radius), Composer Block + Conductor's Podium: up to `composerMax` 8 Composer Blocks in a row, each tapped (BUILD) for its note (the member's chromatic range; the snare picks from the bells'), a podium at the row's end shows the melody on a staff: PERFORM it (TOUCH: tap the names; snare: one steady hit a note) = the row is POWERED (it glows) and opens doors within `doorWire`; ▶ LISTEN plays it with `Sfx.piano` only while nothing listens (the one pitched sound; the first Composer Block's card calls it the composing corner).
- SURVIVAL: 5 hearts drawn as whole notes (no hunger). NIGHT CLAMS hop at you with a note bubble (INSTRUMENT: play that note nearby; TOUCH: tap it; snare: a count) → a Pearl (dropped, see WORLD DROPS); SOUR WISPS drain ½ heart every `wispDrainS` when close (INSTRUMENT: hold any steady in-tune note near one; TOUCH: its key question; snare: a roll) → Pitch Dust (Stage Lamps need it); RUSHERS (from night `spawn.rushersFrom` 3) charge, and a 2-beat rhythm card opens: match it before it arrives → a Valve Spring. FAIRNESS: spawn caps per night (`perNight`, `atOnce`), never within `spawn.safe` tiles, never in light, never inside a closed room with a door (`BW.room`), creatures freeze while `Pitch.isSuppressed`, and THE FAIRNESS CHECK slows a creature until its challenge × `fair.margin` fits the time it needs to reach you. LOSING ALL HEARTS: back at the Practice Cot (else the spawn point), full hearts, tools and the world kept, `dropShare` 25 % of each carried material in a floating BAG where you fell (walk into it). First-time cards (welcome, the first challenge, the first night, each creature, the first Composer Block) are `UI.intro` and pause the world (`gameData.seen`).
- WORLD DROPS AND THE PICKUP RADIUS (game.js `dropItem` / `stepDrops`): a mined block's item pops out of the broken block, and a calmed creature's (Night Clam → Pearl, Sour Wisp → Pitch Dust, Rusher → Valve Spring) out of its spot, as a WORLD ITEM (a small icon: a little arc, then gravity onto the nearest solid ground below, never inside a block, bobbing gently there; still with reduced motion); INSTRUMENT mode's bonus = more items (2, `dropSpread` apart). Nothing goes straight into the bag any more (crafting output, the Locker, your bag and the Survival Nights kit still do). Every drop within `pickupRadius` 1.5 tiles of your body (after its 250 ms pop) is yours; within `magnetRadius` 2.5 it glides to you first (`magnetSpeed`); collecting = `bt-pickup` + the "+1 Pearl" label. A FULL BAG (`invSlots` 24 different items, tools aside, or one item at its limit: `canHold`) leaves it on the ground with "Bag full!" (at most every `bagFullToastS`). Off screen a drop despawns after `dropDespawnS` 600 s (its `t` counts only while off screen, so never one you can see); the lost-hearts BAG is separate and never does. At most `maxDrops` 80: the oldest beyond that joins the nearest drop of its kind (`capDrops`: nothing is lost). Milestones that count creatures still count at the calming moment, not at pickup; the fairness check and creature counts are unchanged.
- LITTLE TOUCHES (reduced motion / the MOTION switch: no swing, chips, poof or drifting text; only a fade or nothing): PICKUP LABELS ("+1 Maple", `pickupLabel` / `drawLabels`, on the sharp overlay after the light with a dark outline): every gain (drops, crafting, the Locker, your bag, a Snack Bag eaten = "+2 Hearts") shows the item's icon + "+n Name" over your head (INSTRUMENT's bonus is in the number: "+2 Tone Shard"), drifting up and fading over `pickupLabelMs`; the same item within `pickupMergeMs` counts up in one label; at most `pickupLabelsMax` 4, stacked upward; the same words go to the `aria-live` region `#btLive`, gathered, at most every `pickupAriaMs`. TOOLTIPS on every item (`[data-item]`: the hotbar, the inventory, a Band Locker, the Recipe Book, the Measure's slots and materials; `#btTip`): hover or keyboard focus shows it after `tipDelayMs`, a touch long-press (`tipLongPressMs`) shows it and lifting hides it (that tap then picks nothing; a normal tap still selects/places); the name, the item's `desc` (recipes.js, one line for 6th graders), "Found: …" for materials (`found`), "Can mine: …" for tools (from the BLOCKS' tiers); it flips above/below/right/left to stay on screen, never covers its item, and opens through `UI.layer` (above every panel). NO REACH CIRCLE: a tile in reach shows the target highlight on hover/aim; a tap on a block OUT of reach outlines it faintly in red for `farFlashMs` with "Too far: walk closer!" (at most every `farToastS`). THE SWING (`swing` / `drawSwing`, `swingMs` 260): mining, tapping a creature or a block's challenge passed turns the avatar to the target and arcs its tool (the item's own icon per tier) from behind the head down toward it; no tool = a quick reach; a new swing restarts it; a failed answer = no swing. A block breaking throws `chips` (4–6 squares in its color, ≤ 300 ms). THE POOF (`poof`, `poofMs` 450): a calmed creature shrinks into a soft puff of cloud in its colors with a few notes floating up (never a flash, never bright white) + `bt-poof` (sounds.js, `play: true`, falls back to `bt-calm`). `Arcade.Blocktave.state()` adds `drops`, `labels`, `tip`, `slots`, `fx` ({swings, chips, poofs, labels, tips, picked, swing, far, rm}); demo `drop`, `calm`, `canHold`, `fromBook`, `addToSlot`. Tests: tests/blocktave.spec.js (labels, tooltips, reach, swing/poof/reduced motion, the Measure at 4 sizes, drops and the pickup radius).
- SAVING: ONE WORLD PER DEVICE in localStorage `bandarcade.blocktave.world` = `BW.encode` `{v: 2, seed, w, h, chunks (16 columns each, run-length encoded column by column), meta (composer notes, powered rows), lockers, bags, drops ([{x, y, item, n, t}]: v 2; a v 1 save loads with none), spawn, time, nights, survived, player, cot}`; autosaved every `autosaveS` 30 s, on pause, when the page is hidden and on leaving. It is NOT in the Arcade Backup Code (too big: storage.js never sees it). The pause menu: SAVE WORLD TO FILE (downloads `blocktave-world.json`), LOAD WORLD FILE (asks first), NEW WORLD (asks TWICE: "Your whole world will be replaced"), and once per device the note "Your world lives on this device. Save it to a file to keep a copy." Small things live in `gameData('blocktave')` (in the Backup Code): `{mode, ms: {member: {milestone: date}}, stats: {member: {ore, clams, wisps, mined}}, found, seen, otherClef, controls}`.
- STARS COME ONLY FROM MILESTONES (hard rule): `BT_CHAPTERS` 5 × 3 (First Steps: a Wooden Mallet / 10 Tone Ore / a shelter with a door; First Night: survive a night / place a Stage Lamp / calm 5 Night Clams; Workshop: place a Luthier's Bench / a Metronome / a Tuner; Deep Notes: reach the Bass Depths or Treble Peaks / mine a Sustain Stone / dispel 3 Sour Wisps; Encore: the Band Hall (`rules.js bandHall`: a closed room with a door, ≥ `minTiles` inside, its walls ≥ `brickShare` Band Hall Bricks, ≥ `stageMin` Stage Floor under it, a Stage Lamp and a Music Stand inside) / power a Composer row of 8 / the Golden Baton). `award(id)` = `setLevel('blocktave', member, chapter, {stars: its milestones done})`, per MEMBER, each once; mining or building more never adds a star, so the leaderboard's `stars` events come only from milestones. A chapter's third milestone shows its results (KEEP BUILDING / CHAPTERS). THE CURRENT CHAPTER = the lowest one not finished: the in-game goals panel (`#goals`) shows it, a "How?" line (recipes.js `hint`, chapter 1's three) under its first unfinished goal, and "+ N milestones already done in later chapters". A later chapter's milestone done early counts at once ("★ Bonus milestone (Chapter 2): Place a Stage Lamp!"), but its chapter's "complete!" results wait until the player REACHES it (every earlier chapter finished: `checkReached`, `gameData.shownCh[member]`; older saves count every finished chapter as shown); until then its card says "Complete · finished early". The chapter select starts on the current chapter. The Recipe Book always shows `BT_ALWAYS_SHOWN` (Maple Planks, Wooden Mallet, Door) with their ingredients; Tone Ore tapped without a mallet = a first-time card ("Planks, Planks, Cork"). A card's goal list is `.bt-card-goals` (in normal flow under the stars line; NEVER the HUD's absolute `.bt-goals`), the cards grow to fit. The level select = the 5 chapter cards (all open) + SURVIVAL NIGHTS (the Endless card: a fresh unsaved world, `endless` numbers, one life, score = nights survived, Top 5 per member, in `Leaderboard.ENDLESS_GAMES`; no milestones there). 3 ★ on Chapter 5 unlocks the Blocktave Builder hat (head `blocktave`) and the Band Hall background (`bandhall`, bg-scenes.js `S.bandhall`); never at the Token Booth.
- CONTROLS (touch first): the pad ◀ ▶ (`#padMove`), MINE/BUILD + JUMP (`#padAct`; `Arcade.holdGuard`), the 6-slot HOTBAR, INVENTORY, CRAFT; ARRANGE CONTROLS (the Settings panel, touch screens) = `shared/pad-arrange.js`, the same module as Arcade Quest's (saved in `gameData.controls` per orientation). Keyboard: A/D or ←/→ walk, W/Space/↑ jump, click = mine/place (right-click = the other), 1–6 hotbar, E inventory, C craft, B mine/build. While a card is open its keys win (letters answer, Space taps).
- THE NOTE LAYOUT (challenges.js `layoutNotes` / `staffRows`: ONE shared layout for EVERY Blocktave staff with several notes: creature cards, Scale Veins, every note card, "Name this scale", Composer rows and the Conductor's Podium; never per-card numbers; rules.js `staffLead` 40, `staffGap` 56, `staffAccRoom` 20, `staffMinW` 420, `staffTail` 12, `noteMinPx` 14, in staff units: the lines are 16 apart): the first note's box (its ledger lines, or its ♯/♭/♮) starts `staffLead` after the right edge of the clef + key signature (`headRight`, measured with GN Music: clef 60 treble / 66 bass, the first sign 70 / 76, +12 a sign); notes are `staffGap` apart center to center, + `staffAccRoom` in front of an accidental; a staff is at least `staffMinW` wide when the screen has room: 1–3 notes CENTERED in the space after the clef, 4+ SPREAD evenly across it (the gaps only grow). The card grows to fit (`fitCard`: up to 92vw; on ≤ 760 px the sheet along the bottom, its padding trimmed); a notehead is never drawn under `noteMinPx` tall on screen: notes that can't fit across at that size WRAP onto more staff rows (`.bt-srow`, each with its own clef and key signature, all one width; two on a phone, three on the narrowest phones with a long key signature) instead of shrinking; a resize lays the card out again. The current note blue, played notes green and the "3 / 6" counter stay. Tests: every member × the 4 scales + chromatic × 2–8 notes at iPad portrait / landscape / a phone: the first note ≥ `staffLead` after the key signature, neighbors ≥ 8 px apart (accidentals included), noteheads ≥ `noteMinPx`, 2–3 notes centered, 8 notes one row on an iPad and wrapped on a phone.
- LIGHT (world.js `lightMap(w, x0, y0, cols, rows, sky, lamps)`, `BW.light` = one tile of it; rules.js `light`): the sky's light on tiles open to the sky, fading `caveDepth` 4 rows into the ground; a SHAFT (an open column with rock on both sides) lets it down `shaftRows` 6 rows, fading, so a mine shaft dug to the sky is lit by day; light spreads from lit open tiles into the open tiles beside them (× `spread` .75 a tile, `shaftRows` tiles at most) and onto the rock faces around them; nothing underground is darker than `caveMin` .12 (the cave's shape always shows); Stage Lamps `lampRadius`. Creatures spawn where that light is under `dark` .5 (unchanged). DRAWN ONLY (game.js `drawnLight`, never in the spawn check, so lamps still matter at night): THE PLAYER'S GLOW (`playerRadius` 4.5 tiles, `playerGlow` .55 at the body, fading; the Golden Baton `batonGlow` +1 tile: every block around you is readable, ≥ .35 within 4 tiles), open space underground `openLift` lighter than rock, water never under `waterMin`. ORE GLINTS (`drawGlints`, `glint`): Tone/Brass Ore, Scale/Spring Veins, Sustain Stone, Rhythm Rock and Rest Crystals keep a faint still glow of their color in the dark. The target block always gets a dark-edged bright outline and its NAME over it (`targetName`), whatever the light. THE WAY UP (`stepWay`, twice a second): more than `lostDepth` 8 rows below the ground nearby (the median of the 17 columns around: a dug shaft doesn't count) without getting closer to the surface for `lostS` 20 s, an arrow at the screen's edge ("↑ Surface", `drawWay`) points along the shortest OPEN path (`pathUp`: 4-way through non-solid tiles to a tile under the open sky at the ground's level, capped at 6000 tiles), straight up when there is none; after `surfaceAfterS` 60 s underground the pause menu has ↑ SURFACE (`#btSurface`, `goSurface`): you stand on the ground at that path's end, or with no open way "Dig upward with your mallet!" (it never digs). Riser Steps (crafted) climb. Tests: noon 20 rows down with no lamp (≥ .35 within 4 tiles, nothing under `caveMin`), a creature can still spawn at the glow's edge at night, a dug shaft lit by day, the arrow + ↑ SURFACE along a winding tunnel, no open way.
- THE PARALLAX BACKDROP (`blocktave/backdrop.js` = `Arcade.BlocktaveBackdrop.create({col})`, `draw(ctx, view)`, `state()`; rules.js `backdrop`; tokens `--bt-marsh-*`, `--bt-brass-*`, `--bt-canyon-*`, `--bt-cave-*`, `--bt-firefly`, `--bt-dust`, `--bt-clef-hint`, `--bt-bg-night` in theme.css): behind the world, the sky gradient, the stars at night, then THREE LAYERS per biome moving `far` .15 / `mid` .35 / `near` .6 of the camera's sideways movement and `vertical` .1 of its up-and-down (around the usual surface view, so caves don't drag the sky). Original art: REED MARSH far = misty purple hills, a pale moon/sun, the water line; mid = rows of tall reeds and cattails (the ONLY layer that sways, `swayPx` 3 every `swayS` 7 s, in 4 slices, the tops most); near = dark reed silhouettes; + fireflies at night (soft glows, slow drift, no blinking) and a faint shimmer on the horizon. BRASS MOUNTAINS far = jagged golden-bronze peaks (lit slopes) + warm glints on the 5 tallest at sunrise/sunset (`dusk`); mid = rock formations shaped like bell flares and coiled tubing; near = dark rocky ridges. PERCUSSION CANYON far = layered red-orange walls with strata; mid = mesas shaped like drums (rim lines) and stacked cymbals; near = dark canyon edges with rawhide brushes; + dust motes by day. DRAW ONCE: each layer is drawn ONCE per screen size into an offscreen strip that repeats every 1200 CSS px (`SW`), at the world canvas's own pixel size, and copied 1:1 (no smoothing, snapped) each frame; NIGHT TINT (`nightTint` .62, darker and bluer) = a tinted copy of each strip redone only when the tint changes by a step (dusk and dawn), never a per-frame composite. BETWEEN BIOMES the two sets cross-fade over `blend` 8 columns each side of the border (the camera's middle column). UNDERGROUND: the CAVE BACKDROP (dark rock, strata lines, a few crystals in the biome's color; its own parallax `cave` .2 both ways) is copied only behind OPEN tiles under each column's ground (one small 1:1 copy per tile from a 2 × 2 texture), fading in over `caveDepth` rows; the BASS DEPTHS / TREBLE PEAKS (the player's layer) show their clef big and faint (`clefAlpha`). Reduced motion / MOTION off: the layers still follow the camera (position), nothing sways, drifts or twinkles. LOW POWER (`perfWatch`): frames averaging over `slowMs` 20 to draw over `slowFrames` 180 = the far layer only, for good on the device (`gameData.bgLow`; demo `bgLow(on)`). Measured 36.7 fps at 4× CPU throttle with all three layers (the perf test also checks it never dropped to the far layer). `state().backdrop` = `{cam, biomes: [{id, a}], offsets: {far|mid|near: {x, y}}, layers, low, sway, still, night, strips, cave: {drawn, tiles, offset}, clef}`; `Arcade.Blocktave.lightAt(x, y)` / `lightGrid()` (the light as drawn last frame), `surface()`, `state().way` / `targetName`, demo `target`, `bgLow`. Tests: tests/blocktave.spec.js "the parallax backdrop" (each biome's own layers, the cross-fade at a border, 100 px of camera = 15 / 35 / 60 px (10 vertically, cave 20), night tint, the cave backdrop + bass clef, no sway with reduced motion, far-only on a slow device).
- Look: cabinet `shape: 'blocks'` (a stack of blocks on top, a mallet on the kick plate), `screen: 'blocks'` (a canvas screen: the avatar mines a Tone Ore block), marquee scene `blocks` (GN Neon), 3D profile `blocks` (a `blocks` topper), menu background scene `blocks`. Sounds (screen `blocktave`): `blocktave-menu`, `blocktave-day`, `blocktave-night`, `bt-break`, `bt-mined`, `bt-place`, `bt-pickup`, `bt-wrong`, `bt-craft`, `bt-calm`, `bt-hurt` (during play: short, unpitched), `bt-powered`, `bt-milestone`, `bt-night`, `bt-dawn`, `bt-respawn`.
- `?demo`: in a card hold Space = the right note (W wrong), TOUCH tap the answer. `Arcade.Blocktave.state()` / `world()` / `worldJSON()` / `importWorld(text)` / `save()` / `fps()`, `Arcade.Blocktave.demo` (give, tp, find, standBy, put, at, mine, act, place, answer, craft, spawn, hurt, time, award, shelter, canSpawnAt, spawnCheck…), `Arcade.BlocktaveCard.current` for tests: tests/blocktave.spec.js (+ chapter 1 in the game runs).

## Arcade Quest (`arcade-quest/`)
- THE UI KIT (pixel theme `q-theme` in style.css): the overworld pause is the kit's (B / Esc / the pad; `Q.talk.pause` and the touch Menu button are gone; tiles Level/Power/HP/XP/Tokens; CHARMS and BAND extras; TITLE SCREEN asks first; no RESTART; `Q.paused` freezes the loop; `Q.pauseOwnsEsc()`; input ignores keys while `body.ui-modal`); SETTINGS = the kit's panel with Text speed / Dodging / Assist / Edit player registered; EPISODE 1 COMPLETE = `UI.results`; the Test Arena reset, NEW GAME over a save and the Save Jukebox ask with `UI.confirm`. Battles have no pause (they wait for you).
- ARCADE QUEST: THE MYSTERIOUS MICROPHONE = engine + battles + TEST ARENA (`index.html?test`) + EPISODE 1, GHOST NOTES MANOR (complete: intro, manor, mini-boss, final boss, ending, cliffhanger, credits, save codes). On the floor (marquee ARCADE QUEST, only that; the full title "Arcade Quest: The Mysterious Microphone" stays on its own title screen, blurb and credits); its line is games.js `summary(store)` ("Episode 1: 60% · 7 friends", from `save.progress`, which `Q.save.write` keeps up to date: every manor ghost helped 50 %, songBb 10, reginaldAwake 10, atticOpen 10, ep1Done 20) instead of a hi-score (arcade.js shows any game's `summary`). `demoOnly: true` in games.js still hides a game that's being built (`Arcade.ALL_GAMES` keeps every game; `requireInstrument` and Select Player look games up there). `unpitched: true` (the snare plays too), `maxStars: 0`.
- THE STORY (Episode 1 only hints; never reveal more): the arcade's own listening MICROPHONE heard every squeak and cracked note for years and nobody ever played just for it; it believes music is only noise and is pulling the sound out of the cabinets to make the noise stop. Misunderstood, not evil. Hints: static, missing sounds, NPC rumors, WHISPERS (engine/world.js `whisperTick`: every 55–95 s outside the Foyer/Practice Hall, `quest-mic-crackle`, a `QUEST_WHISPERS` line floats by, `mic-shadow` in every window tile; `Q.world.whisperNow()` for tests), the dusty sheet and cable in the attic, and the cliffhanger. Every word is in `data/dialogue.js`: `QUEST_DIALOGUE` (+ `conductor`: his speech before the battle via the enemy's `talk`), `QUEST_CUTSCENES` (`intro`, `ending`, `cliffhanger`: shots `{show, lines ('@conductor '/'@mezzo ' prefixes, {you}), sfx, music, wait, title}`), `QUEST_CUTSCENE_SPEAKERS`, `QUEST_WHISPERS`, `QUEST_CREDITS`. Flags: `seen-intro`, `met-conductor`, `ep1Done`.
- CUTSCENES (`engine/story.js`): `Q.go('cutscene', {id, next})`; `next` = another `{id, next}`, `{credits: true, first}` or `{scene, args}`. Pictures drawn in code per `show`: arcade, glitch, drain (canvas 'saturation' fill = color draining), pull, party, crackle, mic, black. Always skippable (SKIP button / Esc: `Q.skipText()` ends the text box), A = next line; reduced motion = no shake, still static, nobody bounces. NEW GAME = reset + `intro` → the Foyer. Befriending the Conductor (world.js `afterBattle`) sets `ep1Done`, `Q.save.achievements()`, then `ending` → `cliffhanger` → scene `credits` (a CSS roll; reduced motion = a static list + DONE) → scene `complete` (EPISODE 1 COMPLETE: stats, save code, `A.Skins.announce`, KEEP EXPLORING → the attic / TITLE).
- THE GHOST CONDUCTOR (`data/enemies.js` id `conductor`, sprite `conductor` 48 × 48 in `sprites-story.js`, attic `boss`, `music: 'quest-boss'`, `talk: 'conductor'`): `stages` [{until (share of HP where the next starts), say, challenge + notes/count/hold/taps/time/bpm/cutoff, dodge}] (battle.js `stage()`/`foe()`/`stageStart()`; `quest-boss-phase` between stages): PLAY first5 (+ the B♭ Blast) → LONG TONE `cutoff` (`hold: [3, 5]`, a secret length, the bar measured against a longer one) → ARTICULATE `bpm: 96` (a silent flashing baton light; attacks within a quarter beat count more). `mustHarmonize` + `finale` lines at HP 1 fill CALM; `harmonizeKeep` (a missed HARMONIZE keeps CALM); `harmonize: {type: 'scale'}` = the whole concert B♭ scale in order (snare: 8 strokes). Rewards the `baton` item (`keep: true` in items.js: never used up, once per battle, `slow: 0.5`). Dodge kinds added in dodge.js: `baton` (warning line, then a sweep around a corner; point-to-segment hit test), `measure` (a row of falling measures with a gap; `rect` shots), `sweep` (fermatas on a sine wave). Keep it warm: he's furious about silence, never cruel.
- SAVE CODES (`shared/backup.js`, loaded by the floor, Select Player and Arcade Quest): alphabet `23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ` (no 0/O/1/I), spaces/dashes/lower case ignored. QUEST CODE v1 = 25 chars (5 × 5), 125 bits: version 4 · level 6 · xp 9 · tokens 11 · `QUEST_V1.items` × 3 · `roster` × 1 · `flags` × 1 · `ghosts` (room:key) × 1 · jukebox 3 · stars converted 11 · pad to 110 · CRC-32 check 15. QUEST CODE v2 = 40 chars (8 × 5): the v1 fields (106 bits, version 2) · `QUEST_V2.charms` owned × 1 (8 places) · equipped 3 + 3 · `QUEST_V2.cosmetics` unlocked × 1 · worn × 1 · pad to 185 · check 15; loading one gives the charms, makes the listed avatar items owned (`ownItem`) and puts the worn ones back on. Those lists ARE the format: never reorder/remove, only append within the padding; a new episode adds a version and keeps reading v1 and v2. QUEST CODE v3 = 45 chars (9 × 5): the v2 layout (version 3) with `QUEST_V3.cosmetics` (= V2's 30 + 13 newer Token Booth items) · pad to 210 · check 15. QUEST CODE v4 = 60 chars (12 × 5): the same layout (version 4) with `QUEST_V4.cosmetics` (= V3's list + the Tumblers) · pad to 285 · check 15 (FROZEN). QUEST CODE v5 (what `Q.save.code()` makes now; THE SHARED WALLET can pass 2047) = 65 chars (13 × 5): version 4 · level 6 · xp 9 · TOKENS 16 · items · roster · flags · ghosts · jukebox 3 · stars converted 16 (= 116 bits) · charms 8 + 3 + 3 · `QUEST_V5.cosmetics` (= V4's list; new {shop} items go at its END; room for 90) unlocked × 1 · worn × 1 · pad to 310 · check 15 (`questEncode(save, {version: 4})` still makes the old layout, for tests). Each version reads with its OWN frozen list and field sizes, so v1–v5 all load and an old code decodes exactly as before (tests/avatar-items.spec.js + tests/fixtures/quest-codes.json: v1–v4; v5: tests/prize-counter.spec.js). Loading a code puts its tokens and `convertedLeft` in the wallet through `Arcade.Tokens.restore`. `Q.save.code()` / `Q.save.fromCode(code)` (engine/save.js); shown at every Save Jukebox (`Q.talk.showCode`; the Foyer's and the Attic's, both in `QUEST_V1.jukeboxes`; the jukebox's menu: SAVE HERE · YOUR BAND · NOT NOW), ENTER SAVE CODE on the title (`Q.talk.enterCode`, asks before replacing). `convertedLeft` = a code's turned-in stars, spread over this device's star sources the next time either counter counts them (`Tokens.starSources`). ARCADE BACKUP (everything: `A.store.exportAll()`/`importAll()` in storage.js) = `BKP` + format `A` + `Z` (CompressionStream deflate-raw) or `R` + base-32 bytes + 7 chars of CRC-32; `A.Backup.open()` (the BACKUP / RESTORE panel: make + COPY, the short Quest code, paste + RESTORE → "This will replace this device's progress. Continue?" → reload), `A.Backup.button(el, cls)` (the Settings panel's "Your progress" group on the floor, Select Player's `#backupBtn` under the card); also opened from Arcade Quest's ENTER SAVE CODE (Backup) and the Save Jukebox's code panel (Backup), and the app's first-launch BRING YOUR PROGRESS (`.app-backup`). The panel is a `UI.layer` (z 80, on top of whatever opened it; focus on MAKE MY BACKUP CODE, Tab stays inside, Esc closes, focus back to the opener; its RESTORE confirmation above it); the overlay scrolls, and on an iPad the on-screen keyboard (visualViewport) pads the panel and scrolls the focused box into view. A typo = `A.Backup.BAD` ("That code doesn't look right. Check each letter.").
- Everything hangs off `Arcade.Quest` (Q). Engine (`engine/`): `core.js` (a 320 × 180 canvas `Q.W × Q.H`, pixelated + letterboxed; HTML UI in `#ui` sized with CSS `var(--px)` = one game pixel; rAF loop that stops while the tab is hidden; `Q.scenes[name] = {enter, exit, update, draw}` + `Q.go(name, args)` (title, arena, battle; later overworld/cutscenes); `Q.css(token)`, `Q.shake` (never under reduced motion), `Q.wait` (game time)), `input.js` (six buttons up/down/left/right/a/b from arrows/WASD, Enter/Space/Z, Esc/Backspace/X, and the on-screen D-pad + A/B shown on touch screens only (`Q.mountPad`: no text in the buttons (drawn arrows, CSS-content letters), `Arcade.holdGuard(pad, {lock, touch})`; the D-pad is ONE captured pointer whose direction follows the finger's angle from its center (drifting off keeps walking, sliding onto another arrow turns, the middle keeps the last direction), A and B their own captured pointers, so a held direction + a tapped A both work; the whole `.q-wrap` has `holdGuard` too); `Q.input.on(fn)` newest first, return true to stop older listeners; `blocked` while a challenge listens so demo keys reach pitch.js), `sprites.js` (`Q.draw`, `Q.spriteEl`, `Q.playerId(member, {tone})`), `text.js` (`Q.say(lines, {name, portrait})` typewriter at the TEXT SPEED setting; `Q.menu(el, items, {cols, onPick, onBack, keys})`; `Q.text(key, vars)`), `save.js` (`Q.save` = one slot in `store.gameData('arcade-quest').save`, v5 = `{v, route (the story ghosts' routes), band (the BAND's choice, null = default), level, xp, hp, maxHp, tokens, items, roster, battles, world (last jukebox {map, x, y, dir}), flags, done ({'<room>:<key>': 'befriend'|'fade'}), converted ({'m:<member>'|'g:<game>': stars}), charms ({owned: {id: true}, equipped: [id|null, id|null]})}`, `Q.save.flag/setFlag/helped()`; bump `SAVE_VERSION` and add an `upgrade()` step when the shape changes; `Q.settings` {textSpeed, dodge 'easy'|'normal', assist, tone} + the SETTINGS panel, whose sound sliders are `Sfx.mountControls`).
- OVERWORLD (`engine/world.js`, scene `world`): rooms from `data/maps/manor.js` (`QUEST_TILES` legend: sprite + solid; `QUEST_MAPS[id]` = `{name, music, tiles: [rows], doors: [{at, to, spawn, dir, need?, locked?}], npcs: [{id, at, sprite?, moved: {flag, at}}], enemies: [{key, type, at, happy?, wander, size?}], things: [{at, say?, use?: 'jukebox'|'booth'|'shop'}], fog?, practice?, safe?, start?}`). Tile-by-tile movement (0.16 s a step), the still tiles prerendered to one canvas per room, animated tiles (2+ frames) drawn every frame, camera clamped to the room, y-sorted actors. Ghosts are visible and wander within `wander` tiles of home (drifting toward you within 3 tiles); a bump starts `Q.go('battle', {enemy, overrides, back: {scene: 'world', args: {resume: true}}})`; battle.js sends back `result {kind: 'befriend'|'fade'|'rest', enemy}`; helped ghosts go in `save.done` (never in a `practice` room) and never return; 'rest' (out of breath) = back to the last jukebox. A = talk/inspect the faced tile, B = pause. `?demo&warp=<room>`; `Q.world.state()`/`here()`/`warp(x, y, dir)` for tests. Never add random encounters.
- TALK (`engine/talk.js`): `data/dialogue.js` = `QUEST_DIALOGUE[id] = {name, sprite, area, speakers?, talk: [{if, set, lines | cycle, do, yes, no}]}` (the first `talk` whose `if` passes: `'flag'`, `'!flag'`, `'helped>=8'`, `'snare'`; `'@speaker '` line prefix; vars `{you}`, `{need}`) + `QUEST_SIGNS[id] = [lines]`. Every line fits the text box (≤ 2 lines, about 80 characters: keep it that way). `do`: `'shop'` (items with a `price`), `'booth'` (Token Booth Terry, same look and dialogue as always, but EVERY number comes from THE SHARED WALLET `Arcade.Tokens` (shared/tokens.js, see THE PRIZE COUNTER): the star sources + TURN IN (`Tokens.starSources/turnIn`, `RATE` 5 a star, `converted` so a star converts once at either counter), the balance, prices and buying (`Tokens.buy(key, {counter: 'quest'})`); its shelves: PLAYER ITEMS = `Tokens.catalog()` (bust thumbnails, the MANOR COLLECTION first with a "MANOR COLLECTION: only here!" tag (`.q-manor`), the Prize of the Week with "⭐ WEEKLY" (`.q-weekly`) and its old price crossed out, OWNED once owned at either counter, bought = owned + worn at once) and CHARMS = `QUEST_CHARMS` with a `price` (unchanged; `Tokens.spend`); battle rewards (`Tokens.add`), Rusty's shop and the HUD/pause/results tiles (`Tokens.balance()`) go through the module too), `'teach'` (the Butler: `Q.challenge.blast(true)`, pass = acc ≥ 0.75 → flag `songBb`), `'wake'` (Sir Reginald: flag `reginaldAwake` + move). The jukebox saves `save.world` and refills HP. Story flags: `songBb`, `reginaldAwake` (helped ≥ 8), `atticOpen` (the Phantom Fermata's `opens`), `atticPassage` (its `opensIfFaded`), `shiftHint`.
- CHARMS (POWER-UPS, ARCADE QUEST ONLY): `QUEST_CHARMS` in `data/items.js` (id never renamed; `effect` `{maxHp}` | `{calm}` × CALM gains | `{dodge}` × enemy note speed | `{block}` hits per battle | `{inTune, at}` × damage when a PLAY's acc ≥ at | `{crit}` a lucky crit's chance; sources `price` (Token Booth), `found: '<room>'` (a map thing with `charm: '<id>'`, `found: '<sign>'`; it twinkles until taken), `reward: '<enemy id>'` (befriending)). Today: Golden Mouthpiece (+8 HP, 150), Lucky Reed (×1.3 CALM, the Kitchen's reed jar / 250), Metronome Charm (×0.85, the Phantom Fermata), Silver Mute (block 1, 200), Tuning Fork (×1.25 at 90 %, the Library), Echo Chime (+4 HP, ×1.15 CALM, the Hidden Passage only: the defeat route's treasure; 6th of the save code's 8 charm places), Sharp Ear (critical hits 1 in 6 instead of 1 in 12, `{crit}`, 300 at the Token Booth; 7th place). `Q.charms` (engine/save.js): `list/get/owned/equipped/effects/mult(k)/sum(k)/give(id)/equip(slot, id|null)/fixHp(s)` (maxHp = `maxHpAt(level)` + charms; level-ups call it). Menu (B) → CHARMS = `Q.talk.charms()` (2 slots, "ARCADE QUEST ONLY", both full = the older comes off). Only battle.js (`addCalm`, damage, `B.mute`, rewards) and dodge.js (`charm`, `mute`) read them: NEVER let a charm touch another game, stars or scoring.
- ALTERNATE ROUTES (befriend OR defeat, both reach the end of Episode 1, neither can soft-lock): an enemy with `opens` (a flag set when befriended) also has `opensIfFaded` (a flag set when DEFEATED) opening a DIFFERENT way to the same place. The Phantom Fermata: befriended = `atticOpen` (the attic door, L); defeated = `atticPassage`: the Attic Stairs' CRACKED WALL (tile K, a door with `need`; open = tile O) leads into THE HIDDEN PASSAGE (room `passage`: the orchestra's backstage, its own signs and Mezzo lines, the ECHO CHIME charm found only there, a secret stair up to the attic's own cracked wall). The first gate ghost a student defeats says "Something shifted somewhere in the manor…" (world.js afterBattle, flag `shiftHint`). The Ghost Conductor's `finale` holds him at 1 HP ONCE (CALM full + `finaleChoice`), then HARMONIZE = the best ending (`ending`: the full party, the Baton, bigger rewards) or PLAY on = he fades: the shorter DEFEAT ENDING (`ending-fade`, show `rafters`); both set `ep1Done` and roll the cliffhanger, credits and EPISODE 1 COMPLETE (its words follow the route). The save's `route` (v5) = {fermata, conductor: 'befriend'|'fade'} (for future achievements). SAVE REPAIR (save.js `routes()`, every load): a gate ghost that is done always has its way open (befriended = `opens`, faded = `opensIfFaded`, unless one is set): old saves, save codes (which carry the ghosts and the roster, not the passage flag) and odd states never lock anyone out. `Q.world.fight(key)` (?demo) starts a room's ghost battle (tests). Tests: tests/quest-routes.spec.js (befriend-all, defeat-all and a mixed run end to end, the repair, the cracked wall).
- EPISODE 1 BATTLES: the B♭ Blast (`songBb`) adds a PLAY submenu: `Q.challenge.blast()` = 4 random concert B♭ scale notes (snare: 6 strokes), × `RULES.blast` 1.3. Enemy fields: `phases` (the challenge changes each round), `mustHarmonize` (HP stops at 1), `listenBoost`, `music` (`quest-miniboss`), `opens` (a flag set on befriend). The round CALM fills, HP can't drop below 1 (`tooCalm`): the student chooses HARMONIZE or PLAY again. The Tuning Slide item `{boost: 1.5}`.
- SPRITES: every picture is a pixel map in `arcade-quest/sprites.js` (the instruments: `shared/instrument-sprites.js`) (+ `sprites-manor.js`: tiles `tile-*` 16 × 16, NPCs `npc-*` 24 × 28, Episode 1 enemies, the 56 × 40 `fermata`) (`window.QUEST_ART`: rows of characters + a palette of THEME TOKENS, the `--q-*` tokens in theme.css; never a hex color). PLAYERS are the student's AVATAR (Create Your Player: `Q.playerId(member, {avatar})` = `Arcade.Avatar.sprites`, pre-drawn canvases in the sprite defs (`canvases`); see AVATARS above) holding their instrument; sprites.js keeps only what the instrument needs: `SHOULDERS`, `SHAPES` (every instrument's pixel maps), `INSTRUMENT_PALETTE` and `POSES[memberId] = {side, front, play}` (`parts: [[shape, x, y, 'back'|'top'?]]`, `hands`: side `{back, front}`, front `{left, right}`, `[x, y]` or `'rest'`; `play` may be 2 frames for bells/snare). Instruments are HELD in CARRY poses and only at the lips in PLAYING; nudge poses in POSES, never paste an instrument onto the face. Sprite ids per member: `player-<m>` (side carry idle; drawn as `player-<m>-play` automatically while `Q.playing()`: a `.q-chal` panel without VOCAB choices), `-walk`, `-front`, `-front-walk`, `-back`, `-back-walk`, `-play` (`strike`: bells/snare frame 1 on `Pitch.onAttack`, `Q.strikeNow()`); a wheelchair's walk frames roll. The overworld draws side (left = `{flip}`), front (down) and back (up) views; battle/title/arena use `player-<m>`; the challenge panel starts at x 74 so the playing pose shows. `index.html?sprites` = the SPRITE REVIEW sheet (sprite-review.js: every member, pose and frame, 1×/2×/4×, dark/light, live previews, drawn as your avatar, a random one or in a wheelchair; no instrument needed). PNG HOOK: `arcade-quest/art/<sprite id>.png` (frames side by side) replaces a drawn sprite when it exists (not players: they are always the avatar); misses are remembered per tab; skipped on file://; `arcade-quest/art/README.md` (keep it current). Dialogue: `{you}` = the instrument, `{hero}` = the avatar's name (`Q.hero()`; `Q.text` fills it everywhere); keep every line ≤ 2 lines with a long name. SETTINGS has EDIT PLAYER instead of the old skin-tone choice; the title menu has YOUR PLAYER.
- DATA (Mat edits): `data/enemies.js` (`QUEST_ENEMIES`, schema at the top: challenge, notes, happy, calm, harmonize, listen, dodge patterns, rewards, lines; never rename an `id`), `data/items.js` (`QUEST_ITEMS`: heal / shield / slow), `data/battle-text.js` (`QUEST_TEXT`, every battle message). Keep it warm and funny and NEVER violent: enemies fade away grumbling or join your band. Never copy Undertale (no heart/soul cursor, no FIGHT/ACT/ITEM/MERCY, no "determination").
- BATTLE (`battle/battle.js`): HP, enemy HP, CALM 0–100; menu PLAY · SERENADE · LISTEN · ITEM · HARMONIZE (only with full CALM); TWO WAYS TO WIN, about equally long at every level, area and band size (the TURNS TABLE in battle.js's header: LV 1 5/5, LV 5 3/3, LV 10 2/2 turns): DEFEAT = PLAY to 0 HP, BEFRIEND = SERENADE (its happy note, HARMONIZE's challenge shorter (challenges.js `serenade`); the snare: a gentle steady beat; CALM = `RULES.serenadeCalm` 38 × accuracy × the CALM SCALE, NO damage; the band follows your lead: after a SERENADE each companion adds your CALM × its `companion.power` (+ its calm perk) instead of attacking) until CALM is full, then HARMONIZE; THE CALM SCALE (`B.calmScale`) = powerAt(level) / powerAt(1) × `calmRef` 30 / the enemy's HP (area × band size): every CALM gain but LISTEN's (SERENADE, PLAY's small CALM, the companions') grows with level and shrinks with HP exactly like damage; THE SAFETY NET (once per battle, not the final boss, ONLY after at least one SERENADE this battle (`B.serenaded`): a PLAY/Blast-only battle always ends at 0 HP, even though PLAY raises CALM a little): a PLAY that would defeat it with CALM ≥ `netCalm` 50 leaves it at 1 HP ("It's barely standing… but it's listening." + "SERENADE to befriend it, or PLAY to finish it."), the next PLAY defeats it; LISTEN adds "It calms down when you play its happy notes: SERENADE."; the first battle ever says the two ways once (flag `pathsTip`); the Ghost Conductor has `serenade: false` (his finale is the story's befriending moment); CRITICAL HITS (PLAY and the B♭ Blast; battle.js `critOf`, RULES `crit*`): a SKILL CRIT at accuracy ≥ .95 AND speed ≥ .8, always; else a LUCKY CRIT at accuracy ≥ .7 with `critChance` 1 in 12 (the Sharp Ear charm `{crit: 1/6}`); × `critMult` 1.5, a big gold "CRITICAL!" float, a stronger shake (none under reduced motion), `quest-crit`, "A perfect note! CRITICAL HIT!" / "Critical hit!"; the band hits × `critBand` 1.2 that turn (never a crit of its own); PERFECT SERENADE (accuracy ≥ .95): CALM × 1.5, a pink "PERFECT SERENADE!" float, `quest-serenade-perfect` (a soft chime); the turns table in battle.js's header shows their effect (a near-perfect player: about one turn sooner at LV 1); damage = POWER × acc × (0.55 + 0.45 × speed), POWER = `Q.save.powerAt(level)` = round(10 × 1.14^(level − 1)) (LV 1 10 · 5 17 · 10 33; shown as "HP 20/20 · POW 10" in the battle HUD, the pause tiles and the Test Arena; level-up: "Power 15 → 17"; the turns table is in battle.js's header). DEFEAT IS THE STUDENT'S CHOICE: 0 HP always fades the enemy, whatever its CALM; a full CALM offers HARMONIZE and says once per battle "Its CALM is full: HARMONIZE to befriend it, or keep playing to defeat it." (`calmChoice`); `mustHarmonize` (HP stops at 1) is kept for an enemy that must never be defeated, and none uses it in Episode 1: the story ghosts have ALTERNATE ROUTES (below). THE BAND: befriended ghosts with a `companion: {power, perk: {power|calm|heal}, hp?}` (data/enemies.js) play beside you, up to 2 (a DUET or a TRIO; `Q.band` in engine/save.js: `members()` = the save's `band` (v4; null = never chosen = the last two befriended; [] = a solo), `choices()`, `set(ids)`), chosen on the BAND screen (`Q.talk.band()`: the pause menu's BAND, the Save Jukebox's menu, the Test Arena; it marks "In your band", says when the band is on the automatic default (`Q.band.auto()`) and that friends never play against their own kind); THE LINEUP (`Q.band.lineup(enemyId)` → `{band, out, subs}`): a friend of the enemy's own kind sits out and, for that battle only, the next friend available (most recently befriended first, not already in the band, not that kind) steps in, else the band plays short; the battle says so once after its intro ("Wisp won't play against another Wisp, so Squeaker joins you instead!" / "…so it's a duet this time." / "…a solo this time.") and the band slides in beside you as it opens (none under reduced motion); a NEW FRIEND on the automatic band: "Wobble joins your band!
