(Change your band anytime: MENU → Band.)", on a band the student picked (kept as it was): "Wobble is ready to join: MENU → Band."; in battle each has its own HP bar and name (HP = your level's max HP × `hp` .6), plays after each of YOUR PLAYs for companion.power × your POWER × YOUR accuracy (+ its perk × your accuracy; a missed PLAY = they wait), and the enemy's turn aims at one member at random (you or a companion still playing; you still play the dodge; the hits go to that member); a companion at 0 HP is "out of breath" and sits out the rest of that battle (never lost; everyone refills after every battle). FAIR FIGHTS: enemy HP × (1 + .3 per companion, a boss .45) and HP/atk × its area's `QUEST_AREAS` (data/enemies.js). All their words: data/battle-text.js. Tests: tests/quest-battle.spec.js (incl. SERENADE → HARMONIZE vs PLAY at LV 1 / 10, solo and trio, the safety net, companions' CALM). enemy turn = a DODGE (`battle/dodge.js`: a white box, a glowing note cursor, patterns rain/side/aimed/wave/burst from data; Easy = ×0.7 speed, ×1.45 spacing; Metronome slows; Cork Grease blocks; ASSIST halves damage). Rewards: XP (level-ups raise max HP), tokens, items; befriend > fade. HP 0 = "out of breath", nothing lost.
- CHALLENGES (`battle/challenges.js`): play (notes ONLY from `buildSequence`), longtone (cents from `reading.midi` vs the target), articulate (`Pitch.onAttack`), vocab (`ancient-ninja-scrolls/vocab.js`, rank 3), fingering (`shared/fingerings.js` + `shared/diagrams.js`; its CSS is `shared/diagrams.css`), harmonize (the enemy's happy note = first-five index `happy`, held; articulate type or snare = clean hits). `resolve()` falls back when an instrument or module can't: snare → articulate (vocab stays), bells no longtone, no fingering chart → play. Each result `{acc, speed, correct, total, success}`.
- THE MIC LISTENS ONLY DURING A PLAYING CHALLENGE: `Arcade.Pitch.pauseListening(true)` from start-up; `Q.listen(on)` flips it for PLAY/LONG TONE/ARTICULATE/HARMONIZE only (`Pitch.listening()` is false while paused, so sounds in menus and dodges never suppress anything), shows "The mic is listening", and calls `Sfx.sync()`. Music: `Sfx.setMusic(name)` (the music manager) plays that FILE only, starting it as soon as it has loaded, the FIRST entry of a room included (main.js preloads quest-title/-foyer/-manor/-battle; each room preloads its ghosts' battle music), (no built-in chiptune): `quest-title`, `quest-intro`, `quest-foyer`, `quest-manor` (a room's `music`: now the FALLBACK), EPISODE 1 ROOM MUSIC = `quest-room-<room id>` for each of the 8 rooms (foyer, hall, library, ballroom, kitchen, stairs, attic, practice; `resume: true`), chosen by `arcade-quest/data/music.js` `QUEST_ROOM_MUSIC` (room → track; several rooms may share one) with the map's `music` as the fallback until the file exists (world.js `musicFor`/`roomMusic`: `setMusic([track, fallback], {fade: 1})`; the rooms behind a room's doors are preloaded); the Sound Board lists them in "Arcade Quest — Episode 1 Rooms", `quest-battle`, `quest-miniboss`, `quest-boss`, `quest-victory`, `quest-cliffhanger`, `quest-credits`; it plays while exploring, in menus and dodges and stops while listening. Sounds: `quest-*` events in sounds.js (screen `arcade-quest`); `quest-hurt` is the only "during play" one; overworld: `quest-step` (quiet, every other step), `quest-door`, `quest-save`, `quest-encounter`, `quest-tokens`; finale: `quest-static`, `quest-mic-crackle` (never while listening), `quest-boss-phase`.
- TITLE: while the audio is still locked (`!Sfx.unlocked`), the title menu waits behind a PRESS START button ("Tap anywhere" / "Press any key"): the first gesture unlocks the audio and starts `quest-title`, and the menu appears just after it (so the same tap can't also press NEW GAME). Returning to the title later shows the menu at once.
- SPRITES for the finale: `sprites-story.js` (`conductor` 48 × 48 idle/downbeat/happy built with small grid helpers `line`/`dot` + a mirrored half; `mic-big` 32 × 56, the microphone's silhouette with its one red light; `mic-shadow`; `fermata-sm`; `baton-tip`).
- THE MENU AND THE KEYS: THE ☰ MENU BUTTON = the kit's pause button placed in the game screen's top-right corner (`#qMenuSlot` in `#stage`, beside `#ui`; `.q-menubtn`, ≥ 44 px), shown only while you're free to walk (world.js `update`: no battle, conversation, panel or listening challenge); it opens the pause menu, and so do B, Esc, P and M (world.js, a capture-phase listener; M again closes it); ARRANGE CONTROLS keeps the pad clear of it (it's one of controls.js's obstacles). KEYBOARDS ONLY (`!Q.input.touch`; engine/input.js `Q.keyHints`): THE KEY LEGEND (`#qKeys`, bottom-left while exploring: "Arrows: move · Z / Enter: A (talk, choose) · X / Esc: B (back) · M: Menu · H: hide hints" + a ✕ button, `.dim` after `FADE_MS` 10 s without a key, back on a key, hover or a new room/scene; Settings "Show key hints" = `Q.settings` `keyHints`, default On; H (in the world, no panel open) or the ✕ flip that same setting, saved, with a toast "Key hints hidden: press H to show them." / "Key hints on." (`Q.keyHints.set(on)`)), the text box's "▼ Enter" (`keyHints.more`), `keyHints.label('a')` = "A (Z / Enter)" (touch: "A"), PRESS START's "Press Enter (or any key)", and ONE line the first time the game is played on a keyboard (after the opening story; `gameData.keysTip`): "On a keyboard: Z or Enter = A, X or Esc = B, M = Menu." Settings lists every key under "Controls" (`CONTROLS` in save.js). ONE SELECTION LOOK in every Arcade Quest menu (`Q.menu`, engine/text.js; style.css `.q-btn.sel`): the selected button is FILLED (`--q-cursor`, dark text) with a ▶ pointer in the gap to its left (absolutely placed, hopping in steps; still with reduced motion), and the selection never changes any button's size (battle buttons one fixed height, a smaller label in 5-option `.q-five` rows; "What will you do?" has its own space above them; ITEM's list `.q-items` grows to fit each description); unselected buttons are all dark with a grey border; an action's identity is a thin stripe at the TOP inside it (PLAY gold, SERENADE pink; the level-select main button `.ls-main` gold the same way), never a border; HARMONIZE ready = pink letters + a pink pulse, and the fill wins when it's selected; the MOUSE MOVES THE SELECTION (pointerenter; a disabled button can't take it), focus and Tab too; a plain `.q-btn` outside a menu fills on hover. LABELS NEVER CLIP (style.css `.q-bl`, `.q-hname`, `.q-cname`; engine/text.js `Q.fitText(el)`): every label has line-height 1.35 + a little padding (the GN Quest glyphs are taller than a 1.0 line box) and no overflow:hidden or ellipsis; one too wide for its button shrinks a step at a time (`Q.FIT`: ×0.92 down to 65 %, never under 9 px), then wraps to 2 balanced lines (`.q-wrap2`); a fixed-height button that still overflows shrinks its description (`small`) first, then the label. Q.menu fits every label as it builds a menu, the battle HUD's names as a battle opens, and core.js `resize` refits every `.q-fit` (`Q.refit(root)`), as does a font arriving. Tests: tests/quest-labels.spec.js (every menu, phone / iPad both ways / laptop). THE CHOSEN OPTION in every Settings toggle is the cursor color + a ▶ + an inner border (style.css: the quest's `.ui-seg>button` rule has the kit's specificity, so the pressed rule must follow it). Tests: tests/quest-menu.spec.js.
- NO ZOOM + ARRANGE CONTROLS (`engine/controls.js` on top of `shared/pad-arrange.js`, the module Blocktave's pad uses too; after input.js; `.q-resetview`, `.q-arrange`, `.pad-free` in style.css): the viewport meta has maximum-scale=1; the stage and the pad are touch-action: none (the pad's touchstart/touchend cancelled), every other button touch-action: manipulation, Safari's gesturestart/gesturechange and dblclick are blocked. If the page is zoomed anyway (visualViewport.scale > 1.05, checked on every visual-viewport change and when the game regains focus) a RESET VIEW button shows (sized for the zoomed view): user-scalable=no for a moment, then back, scrollTo(0, 0), layout again (`Q.view.check(scale)/reset()/state()`). ARRANGE CONTROLS (the SETTINGS panel, touch screens only: `Q.input.touch`): an edit mode over the game (drag the D-pad and the A/B cluster anywhere, kept fully on screen; D-pad size and A/B size 75–150 %, opacity 20–100 %; RESET TO DEFAULT; DONE); the pad presses nothing meanwhile (`Q.input.arranging`). Saved per device AND orientation in `gameData('arcade-quest').controls` = `{portrait|landscape: {dpad: {x, y, s}, ab: {x, y, s}, op}}` (centers as shares of the window). The page layout never changes: an arranged cluster is only moved and scaled with a transform from its normal place (`.q-wrap.pad-free`), re-placed by core.js `layout()` after every resize/turn. It NEVER COVERS a menu (any `.q-menu`: the battle's PLAY · LISTEN · ITEM · HARMONIZE), a text box or a challenge's buttons: a MutationObserver on #ui nudges it to the nearest free spot while one shows, back to its saved spot after. Keyboard play is unchanged. `Q.arrange.open()/close()/state()`. Tests: tests/quest-controls.spec.js.
- THE PAGE FITS THE VISIBLE AREA (engine/core.js `layout()`): `--app-h` = the visual viewport's height (100dvh / 100vh before JS), redone on resize, orientation change and `visualViewport` resize/scroll (iPad Safari's toolbar showing or hiding, Split View, the keyboard); safe-area padding on `.q-wrap` (viewport-fit=cover). With the touch pad showing: LANDSCAPE (wider than tall) = `.pad-side`: D-pad on the left, A/B on the right (B lower-left of A), both at the bottom of the visible area, the 16:9 game centered between them; PORTRAIT and tall Split View = the pad under the game. Button size `--pb` = 44–58 px from the visible height (A/B 1.3×). Never size anything in this game with plain `vh`.
- Font: `GN Quest` = Pixelify Sans (OFL, `shared/fonts/pixelify.woff2`, Latin + Latin-1), the game's text font (large sizes); ♭/♯ fall back to the music font.
- `?demo`: hold Space = the right note(s), W = wrong, S = steady long tone, T (or Space) = an attack, V (F) = auto-answer VOCAB/FINGERING. `Q.battleState()`, `Q.dodge.state()`, `Q.cutscene()` for tests. `?demo&warp=attic` = the Ghost Conductor.
- STAFFS in challenges are SVG in `#ui` (never on the canvas), so they render at full resolution: `.q-staff svg line` 2.6 px, stems 3 px, outlined heads (style.css). Keep them large.
- Cabinet: `shape: 'quest'` (stepped pixel silhouette), `marquee: 'quest'` (8-bit microphone, `A.QUEST_MIC`), `screen: 'quest'` (glitch static); 3D profile `quest`.

## TUNE UP (TOOLBOX) (`note-checker/`)
- THE TOOLBOX (`toolbox.js`, loaded BEFORE the tools: `requireInstrument('note-checker')` + `mountTopbar`, `Arcade.TuneUp` = `{inst, member, tab, data() (= gameData('tuneup')), save(), register(id, {listens, enter, leave, micOn}), go(id), reward(title, text), state()}`): three TABS at the top (`.tu-tabs`, role tablist, ≥ 48 px, tinted cyan / pink / yellow, ←/→ Home/End): NOTE CHECKER (`checker.js`) · TUNER (`tuner.js`) · METRONOME (`metronome.js`); styles `style.css` (the checker) + `tools.css`, tokens `--tu-*` in theme.css. The last tab is remembered (`gameData('tuneup').tab`); `?tool=checker|tuner|metronome` opens one; the top bar's TUNE UP button opens the toolbox. SWITCHING TABS calls the old tool's `leave()` (its sound stops: the metronome's kit, the tuner's tone; its range/a4/demo keys put back), then the mic: a LISTENING tab (the Note Checker; the Tuner for pitched instruments) = `requireMic` → `Pitch.pauseListening(false)` (the tab tap is the gesture); the METRONOME (and the snare's Tuner tab) = `Pitch.stop()`: it never opens the microphone. The mic sensitivity block shows on listening tabs only. Every handler in a tool acts only while its tab shows. TOOLS: no stars, no setLevel, no leaderboard events (only the two avatar items below). `T.reward` = a small panel with `Skins.announce` (nothing new = nothing shows), only between notes / after the click has stopped.
- SNARE DRUM: the TUNER tab says "Drums don't need tuning! Try the Metronome." + a button to it; the METRONOME works for everyone; the NOTE CHECKER keeps ARTICULATION.

### The Note Checker (`checker.js`)
- Modes FIRST 5 | B♭ | E♭ | F | A♭ | CHROMATIC | ARTICULATION (stored as 'five', 'Bb'…'Ab', 'full', 'art'). ARTICULATION: counts `Pitch.onAttack` (big number + flash, note name, per second, Reset, the hold hint); the only mode for the snare (not saved); Mat's check of attack detection on real instruments. Scale modes show the scale up and down with its key signature; a held pitch lights the first dark place with that pitch, octave-exact, "11 of 15 notes".
- FIRST 5 NOTES: the original checker (letter names, any octave; its staff is unchanged). CHROMATIC (FULL RANGE): the member's whole chromatic scale on wrapped staff rows (≥ 50 px per note so accidentals never touch; the notes shared out evenly between the rows), each note captioned. SCALES: the way UP on one row and the way DOWN on the next (each split in two when a row would get under 40 px a note, e.g. on phones). EVEN SPACING (checker.js `drawFull`): every gap between two notes is equal, counting the room a ♯/♭/♮ takes in front of its note (`ACC_L` vs `HEAD_L`), with one gap for every row so the rows line up. Key signatures use `staffSVG(…, {sigStyle: 'big'})` (ui.js `SIG_STYLES.big`: further from the clef, larger ♯/♭; `keySigWidth(sig, 'big')`): only the Note Checker; every other game keeps the normal style.
- The octave rule: a note lights gold only when held in the octave written on the staff (exact sounding pitch via `sounds`). A same-letter note in another octave never counts; if the student replays a found note while its other octave is still missing, show "That's a D, but an octave lower. Try the higher one." Readings an octave outside the whole range are folded in by the pitch engine (mic error), so they do count.
- The note being heard gets a glowing outline; "Going down" shows the same pitches descending with flats (one set of found notes). `?demo` in full range: ↑/↓ pick a note, hold Space to play it, Shift+Space an octave low.

### The Tuner (`tuner.js`): THE HOT-AIR BALLOON
- Numbers in the commented `TUNER` block at its top (Mat edits): `maxCents` 50, `smoothMs` 120, `inTune` 5, `close` 15, `holdS` 4, `drainS` 2, `tipAfterS` 1.5, `wobbleCents` 12, `quietMs`, `rest`, `topRoom` .8, `confettiGapS`, `toneMs` 2000, `listenAfterMs` 400, `rewardHolds` 5.
- THE SCENE: an original evening sky (`--tu-sky-*`), the student's AVATAR in the basket of a striped balloon (`avatarHTML` three times: calm, HAPPY = mouth `beam` + eyes `cheerful`, WORRIED = mouth `surprised` + brows `raised`; `data-face`), a LANDING PLATFORM (a floating island with a flag, `.tn-platform`, 58 % down: its TOP edge is where the basket lands). THE MAPPING: the basket's bottom = the platform − cents/maxCents × (platform − `topRoom` × balloon height) when SHARP (above), + |cents|/maxCents × (bottom − platform) when FLAT (below), clamped; IN TUNE (|c| ≤ inTune) = on the platform. Cents are smoothed (exponential, `smoothMs`); nothing heard for `quietMs` = it drifts back to a resting spot just below the platform + "Play a long note!".
- THE STATES: IN TUNE = landed, happy face, confetti ONCE per landing (≤ one burst per `confettiGapS`, none with reduced motion / MOTION off: the landing is a simple settle), "IN TUNE!" green; CLOSE (≤ close) "Almost! A little lower" / "Almost! A little higher" amber; FARTHER "Too high (sharp)" / "Too low (flat)" coral, worried face, a small wobble, ▲/▼ ARROWS on both sides pointing the way to MOVE. Direction never by color alone (height + arrows + words). `.tn-words` `data-kind`.
- THE NOTE: the big WRITTEN name (the reading's sounding note + the member's `sounds`, spelled like the arcade's parts: `NAMES`), on a small staff in their clef, "concert F" under it; "Show concert pitch first" (`gameData('tuneup').concertFirst`) swaps them; "+12 ¢" in the corner ("Show cents", `showCents`, on by default).
- HOLD IT: a ring around the platform fills while the note stays within `close` (`holdS` to fill), drains over `drainS` when it wobbles out or stops; full = "Steady! 4 seconds in tune" + "Best: 7.2 s" (the longest run within `close` this page load). Each fill (once until the ring empties) adds to `gameData('tuneup').holds['YYYY-MM-DD']` (30 days kept).
- WHAT TO DO: `TUNING_TIPS` (member id → [sharp, flat], Mat edits) after `tipAfterS` sharp or flat (`#tnTip`); a note jumping around (the cents' spread over 2 s > `wobbleCents`) = "Steady air, relaxed embouchure."; bells: "Bells don't need tuning!".
- FREE mode (default) = cents from the nearest note (`reading.cents`). TARGET mode (`tunerMode`, the target picker `tunerTarget`: the TUNING NOTE = concert B♭ nearest the first-five tonic inside the member's range, + the first five) = cents from the TARGET in any octave (`reading.midi`); another note = "That's an A, try a B♭!" (written names); the target's name on the flag. HEAR THE NOTE: TARGET mode, `Pitch.pauseListening(true)` + `Sfx.sync()`, `tones.play([target], {noteMs: 2000})` (shifted by the reference A), `Pitch.suppress` too; when the tone has faded + `listenAfterMs`: listening again, `ignoreCurrent()`, "Now you play it!". The tone's pitch can never count (the mic is paused).
- THE MIC: `Pitch.setRange(member.soundLow, soundHigh)` (octave folding as usual), `Pitch.a4` = the reference A while the tab is open, the sensitivity in the block below and in Settings. Settings → Tuner: "Reference A" 438–445 Hz (`gameData('tuneup').a4`).
- THE GOLDEN TUNING FORK: the 5th full HOLD IT ring of one day → `T.reward` once the note has ended (≥ 1 s of nothing heard), never mid-hold.
- `?demo`: hold Space = in tune, U = sharp (+25 ¢), J = flat (−25 ¢), W = a wrong note; `Pitch.demoJitter` .02 on this tab. `Arcade.TuneUp.tuner.state()` (`status`, `dir`, `cents`, `basket`, `platform`, `pos` rest/landed/above/below, `words`, `arrow`, `big`, `small`, `hold`, `filled`, `best`, `tip`, `mode`, `target`, `phase` listen/tone/yourturn, `face`, `confetti`, `flag`) / `hear()` / `setMode()` / `TARGETS` / `TUNING_TIPS` for tests.

### The Metronome (`metronome.js`): THE AVATAR ON THE BEAT + THE TEMPO LADDER
- NEVER USES THE MICROPHONE (the tab turns it off). Numbers in the commented `METRO` block (Mat edits); THE TEMPO WORDS = `TEMPO_WORDS` ([from BPM, word]: Largo < 60, Larghetto 60–65, Adagio 66–75, Andante 76–107, Moderato 108–119, Allegro 120–167, Presto 168+).
- THE SCHEDULER: the click = music-highway/backing.js's kit (`Sfx.buffer('mh-click')`, else the generated woodblock; unpitched noise) on `Sfx.output()`, scheduled on the AudioContext clock `lookaheadS` ahead by a `tickMs` timer; tick j of a SEGMENT is at `t0 + j × tick length` (never added up), a new segment only when the tempo or the meter changes (a control's tempo on the next beat, the ladder's on a DOWNBEAT). Beat 1 = the accent click (`vol.one`), 6/8's beat 4 accented too (`vol.accent`), other beats `vol.beat`, subdivisions softer (`vol.sub`). SILENT MODE: the same schedule, no click (`sound: false`); the audio clock is still used when the audio is on. Sound off / not unlocked = a performance.now() clock.
- METERS: 2/4, 3/4, 4/4, 2/2 (¢, 2 half-note beats), 6/8 = 6 eighths (accents 1 and 4; "Feel in 2" = 2 dotted-quarter beats with the eighths as soft ticks); BPM = the counted beat. SUBDIVISION none / eighths / triplets / sixteenths (disabled in 6/8).
- THE STAGE: the avatar's full-body FIGHT stills (`AvatarFight.stillHTML`: idle, and VICTORY = arms up), else the bust; the beat counter (accented numbers underlined), beat dots, subdivision ticks. THE BOP follows the AUDIBLE time (`Arcade.AudioClock`: `audAt`): on the ground and squashed at each beat, a bounce in between (`hop`), beat 1 a bigger hop (`hopOne`) with arms up for 60 % of the beat; reduced motion / MOTION off = no bobbing (the counter still lights). Silent mode: the lit number glows gently, at most once per `flashMinMs` (≤ 3 a second), never full-screen.
- CONTROLS: −/+ (±1, held = ±5 after `holdMs`, repeating), the slider 40–208, TAP TEMPO (the average of the last 4 taps; > 2 s idle starts over), the meter, subdivision, SILENT, START/STOP; keys Space start/stop, ↑/↓ tempo (Shift ±5), T tap (never inside a box or the slider); every control ≥ 44 px. A HIDDEN TAB stops it: "Paused — tap START".
- THE TEMPO LADDER (`#mtLad`): START (60), GOAL (100), STEP (4), MEASURES (4), STOP AT GOAL; rungs = start, +step… up to the goal (60→100 step 4 = 11 rungs, 10 steps). START LADDER = a one-measure count-in, then every MEASURES measures +STEP on a downbeat; at the goal "You climbed to 100! 🎉" (`#ldMsg`) and keeps going (or stops after MEASURES measures at the goal); `tuneup-ladder-top` (falls back to `level-complete`) plays once the click has STOPPED, never on a beat. HOLD (stay on this rung: "Practice this speed until it's easy"), STEP BACK (−STEP at the next downbeat; HOLD stays as it was). The ladder graphic (rungs, the avatar's chip climbing), "Step 3 of 11 · 2 measures to the next step". The tempo controls are disabled during a ladder. A ladder of ≥ `ladderPlate` 8 steps climbed to the goal adds 1 to `gameData('tuneup').ladders` (3 = the Ladder Climber name plate, `.av-plate-ladder`).
- SAVED: `gameData('tuneup').metro` {bpm, meter, feel2, sub, silent} and `.ladder` {start, goal, step, measures, stopAtGoal}.
- `Arcade.TuneUp.metronome.state()` / `log()` (every scheduled tick: t, bar, beat, sub, accent, one, vol, bpm, sound, countIn) / `bops()` (each beat's click time vs. the bop's landing on the audible clock) / `start({ladder})` / `stop()` / `setBpm()` / `tap()` / `tempoWord()` / `times(bpm, n)` / `rungs()` for tests.

## Testing
- **Every PR must pass the tests.** New games must pass the smoke and game-run tests; add a tests/games.js entry if the game needs special steps.
- THE AUTOMATIC TESTS (`tests/`, README there; `.github/workflows/tests.yml` runs them on every pull request and push to main, Chromium + WebKit): SMOKE (`smoke.spec.js`: every page in `pages.js` at iPad landscape/portrait and Chromebook sizes: no JS/console error, no missing local file (except the optional files the site probes for: `OPTIONAL` in helpers.js), no outside host, no visible button off the screen sideways), GAME RUNS (`game-runs.spec.js` + `games.js`: every game in shared/games.js from ?demo Level 1 to its results with stars saved, Endless cards to GAME OVER; a game needing other steps gets a STEPS entry), PITCH (`pitch.spec.js`, Chromium: synthesized clarinet/flute/sax/trumpet/tuba tones through `Pitch._analyse`, octave-exact), NETWORK (`network.spec.js`: the leaderboard mocked, only the allowed fields, nothing in ?demo), SAVE (`save.spec.js`: the Arcade Backup Code round trip), AVATAR ITEMS (`avatar-items.spec.js`: old Quest codes v1–v3 read exactly as before, a v4 round trip, the Tumblers and the Grandmaster's Aura) SHOWTIME SNARE (`showtime-snare.spec.js`: every snare job with `Onsets.fake(time, level)`, fairness at every showtime) TUNE UP (`tuneup.spec.js`: the tabs and the mic per tab, the balloon's height/words/arrows, written vs concert names, HOLD IT, the tips, HEAR THE NOTE with the mic paused + TARGET mode, the snare's tab, the metronome's schedule/accents/6-8/subdivisions/tap/tempo words/silent mode/the bop on the audible beat/reduced motion/hidden tab, the Tempo Ladder on downbeats + HOLD + STEP BACK + the goal, the two items) and RHYTHM DOJO (`rhythm-dojo.spec.js`: every Counting Board example, synthesized claps found to the millisecond, the game's own clicks looped back into the clap detector never counting, judging/rests/holds/feedback colors, the timing check, HEAR IT and pause). Run them: `cd tests && npm ci && npx playwright install chromium webkit && npx playwright test`.
- `python3 -m http.server` from the repo root, then open `http://localhost:8000/?demo`.
- Before finishing a change, verify with `?demo` that a level can be completed and that the arcade floor's HI-SCORE for that game updates (it shows only when an instrument is saved).
- `?demo` in the mic games (any NOTES pool) and the Note Checker's scale/chromatic modes: hold Space to play the note on the staff / the glowing note.
- Real-instrument testing is Mat's job. Ask him which instruments/notes misread rather than guessing at detector changes.

## Detector tuning (shared/pitch.js)
YIN with threshold 0.15, clarity ≥ 0.8, loudness gate from the sensitivity slider, ±0.65-semitone hysteresis, 110 ms release gap. Per-instrument search range comes from `range` in `instruments.js` (min × 0.78, max × 2.3); the dip search starts past the edge of a dip whose bottom lies above the search range. Change these carefully and note why in the commit message.
- THE TWELFTH / OCTAVE CHECK (`lowerCandidate`, numbers in `Pitch.LOW`): YIN takes the FIRST low dip, so a strong overtone can win (a clarinet's chalumeau notes: strong 3rd harmonic, weak 2nd, so concert B♭3 read as F5 = written C read as G). After the dip at τ it tries 1.5τ, 2τ, 3τ (a fifth, an octave, a twelfth below; the nearest that passes wins) and moves down only if the lower pitch is inside the search range, YIN's difference there is low too, there is real energy AT the lower pitch (Goertzel: ≥ `LOW.ratio` × the pitch found and ≥ `LOW.floor` × the median of six points beside it) and at least `LOW.supportCount` more of its harmonics the higher note doesn't have (SUPPORT: a clarinet's 5th and 7th). Within `LOW.hum` Hz of 50/60 Hz mains hum the lower fundamental must be strong (`humRatio`). Tested with synthetic tones (odd-harmonic clarinet with an 8–20 % fundamental, flute, oboe, saxes incl. a loud 2nd harmonic, bassoon, trumpet, horn, trombone, tuba; noise and hum): see the PR table. Retune only against that kind of table and Mat's real instruments.
- STABILITY: while a note is held, a different pitch replaces it only after `Pitch.confirmFrames` (2) readings in a row; that frame reports null and the held note stays held (a one-frame jump to an overtone can't count, or restart a hold). From silence a note is taken at once.
- `?pitchdebug` on any page with pitch.js: the PITCH DEBUG overlay (bottom left, ~6×/s): frequency, the note concert and written (the saved member's `sounds`), cents, clarity, level vs gate, the held note, and each candidate (f, f/1.5, f/2, f/3) with its YIN difference, magnitude, the level beside it, harmonics found and why it was or wasn't chosen. `Pitch._analyse(buf, sr)` (tests) runs one buffer through the same analysis (no hysteresis).
